FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# Base44 Project

Use this repository to run and edit the app locally, then publish changes back through Base44.

Any change pushed to the repo will also be reflected in the Base44 Builder.

## Prerequisites

1. Clone the repository using the project's Git URL.
2. Navigate to the project directory.
3. Install dependencies: `npm install`.
4. Install the Base44 CLI: `npm install -g base44@latest`.
5. Install [Deno](https://docs.deno.com/runtime/getting_started/installation/) â€” the local Base44 backend runs on it.

Run `base44 --help` (or see the [CLI reference](https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/commands/introduction)) for the full command surface.

## Run Locally

Three commands, from the project root:

```bash
base44 login   # one-time per machine
base44 link    # one-time per clone
base44 dev     # local backend + frontend together
```

Open the frontend URL that `base44 dev` prints (typically `http://localhost:5173`).

Notes:

- **Every fresh clone needs `base44 link`.** It writes `base44/.app.jsonc` (the app-id pointer), which is deliberately gitignored. Your app id is in the Builder URL (`app.base44.com/apps/<id>/...`); `base44 link --help` shows the non-interactive flags.
- **`base44 dev` runs the frontend for you** (via `site.serveCommand` in this repo's `base44/config.jsonc`) â€” never run `npm run dev` yourself: alone it serves a UI with no backend behind it (`[base44] Proxy not enabled`, every `/api` call fails), and alongside `base44 dev` the second Vite silently takes the next port and you end up looking at the wrong one.
- **The app must be published at least once for the UI to load under `base44 dev`.** The frontend boots by fetching app settings from the hosted app; before the first publish that fails and every page redirects to login. The local API works regardless.
- Entities, functions, and auth run locally â€” entity data is **in-memory only**, wiped when `base44 dev` restarts. Everything else (Core integrations, OAuth login) is forwarded to your deployed app. Full breakdown: [Local development overview](https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview).

## Frontend Only, Hosted Backend

To work on just the frontend against your app's live hosted backend:

```bash
base44 dev --remote
```

âš ï¸ In this mode writes go to your app's **production data** â€” plain `base44 dev` keeps everything local.

## Publish Your Changes

After pushing your changes to git, open the Base44 dashboard and publish the app:

```bash
base44 dashboard open
```

This repo syncs to Base44 through git, so publish from the dashboard rather than `base44 deploy` â€” a CLI deploy ships your local tree directly, bypassing the sync, and the deployed state silently diverges from the repo.

## Docs & Support

GitHub integration: [https://docs.base44.com/developers/app-code/local-development/github](https://docs.base44.com/developers/app-code/local-development/github)

Local development: [https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview](https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview)

Support: [https://app.base44.com/support](https://app.base44.com/support)

FILE: base44/functions/aiStudio/entry.ts
================================================================================
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.54';

const SYSTEM = {
  base: `You are the PRI-ANIME story atelier â€” a senior anime and manhwa writer, world-builder and script editor.
Write with cinematic specificity: concrete images, emotional stakes, sound design notes, camera language.
Never plagiarise existing franchises, characters or studios; every name and world must be original.
Keep continuity across a response. Use clean Markdown with short headings and tight paragraphs.`,
  chat: `Answer as a recommendation concierge and creative partner. When recommending titles, give 3-5 real, existing anime with a one-line reason each, then one original idea the reader could pursue.`,
  story: `Produce an original story treatment. Structure: Logline, World, Cast (3-4 characters with wants and flaws), Three-Act Arc with episode beats, Visual Motif, Closing Image. Aim for 450-700 words.`,
  character: `Produce a character bible: Name, Age & role, Visual design (silhouette, palette, signature prop), Voice & speech rhythm, Want vs Need, Wound, Secret, Relationship web (3 links), Arc sentence.`,
  episode: `Produce an episode outline with a cold open, four numbered acts with timestamps, dialogue samples with speaker labels, and a cliffhanger. Note the emotional temperature of each beat.`,
  dialogue: `Produce a scene script: slugline, action lines in present tense, and dialogue with speaker labels. Add parenthetical direction sparingly. End on a turn of the scene.`,
};

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));

    const mode = SYSTEM[body.mode] ? body.mode : 'chat';
    const language = String(body.language || 'en').slice(0, 5);
    const prompt = String(body.prompt || '').slice(0, 6000).trim();
    const style = String(body.style || '').slice(0, 120);

    const history = Array.isArray(body.messages) ? body.messages.slice(-12) : [];
    const historyText = history
      .filter((message) => message && message.content)
      .map((message) => `${message.role === 'assistant' ? 'ATELIER' : 'DIRECTOR'}: ${String(message.content).slice(0, 3000)}`)
      .join('\n\n');

    if (!prompt && !historyText) {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'Write a prompt first.' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const languageName = { en: 'English', hi: 'Hindi (Devanagari)', ja: 'Japanese', es: 'Spanish' }[language] || 'English';

    const fullPrompt = `${SYSTEM.base}

TASK MODE: ${mode}
${SYSTEM[mode]}

RESPOND IN: ${languageName}
${style ? `VISUAL / TONE STYLE: ${style}` : ''}

${historyText ? `CONVERSATION SO FAR:\n${historyText}\n\n` : ''}DIRECTOR'S BRIEF:
${prompt || 'Continue the conversation with a useful next step.'}`;

    const result = await base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.InvokeLLM({ prompt: fullPrompt });

    const text = typeof result === 'string' ? result : result?.response || '';
    if (!text) {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'The AI returned an empty response. Try again.' }, { status: 502 });
    }
    return Response.json({ ok: true, text, mode, language });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json(
      { ok: false, error: error.message || 'The AI studio is unavailable right now.' },
      { status: 502 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: base44/functions/anilist/entry.ts
================================================================================
import { normalizeAniList, currentSeason, clampPage, clampPerPage } from '../../shared/media.ts';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://graphql.anilist.co';
const TTL = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const cache = new Map();

const CORE = `id title { romaji english native } coverImage { extraLarge large color } bannerImage description(asHtml: false) genres averageScore meanScore popularity episodes duration status format season seasonYear startDate { year month day } studios(isMain: true) { nodes { name } } trailer { id site } nextAiringEpisode { episode timeUntilAiring }`;

const LIST_QUERY = `query ($page: Int, $perPage: Int, $search: String, $genre: String, $season: MediaSeason, $seasonYear: Int, $sort: [MediaSort]) {
  Page(page: $page, perPage: $perPage) {
    pageInfo { total currentPage lastPage hasNextPage }
    media(type: ANIME, isAdult: false, search: $search, genre: $genre, season: $season, seasonYear: $seasonYear, sort: $sort) { ${CORE} }
  }
}`;

const DETAIL_QUERY = `query ($id: Int) {
  Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
    ${CORE}
    duration
    source
    countryOfOrigin
    characters(sort: [ROLE, RELEVANCE], perPage: 8) { nodes { id name { full } image { large } } }
    relations { edges { relationType node { id type title { romaji english } coverImage { large } } } }
    recommendations(perPage: 8, sort: RATING_DESC) { nodes { mediaRecommendation { id title { romaji english } coverImage { large extraLarge } averageScore } } }
  }
}`;

const USER_AGENT = 'PRI-ANIME/1.0 (+https://fierce-prime-anime-core.base44.app)';
const RETRY_DELAYS = [600, 1500];

async function gqlOnce(query, variables) {
  const key = JSON.stringify({ query, variables });
  const hit = cache.get(key);
  if (hit && hit.expires > Date.now()) return hit.value;

  const response = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
    method: 'POST',
    // Identify the app explicitly â€” bare server-side clients get flagged.
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'User-Agent': USER_AGENT,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables }),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000),
  });

  if (response.status === 429) {
    const error = new Error('AniList rate limit reached â€” try again in a minute.');
    error.code = 429;
    throw error;
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const text = await response.text();
    const error = new Error(`AniList returned ${response.status}: ${text.slice(0, 160)}`);
    error.code = response.status;
    throw error;
  }

  const payload = await response.json();
  if (payload.errors && payload.errors.length) {
    const error = new Error(payload.errors[0].message || 'AniList rejected the query');
    error.code = 400;
    throw error;
  }
  if (!payload.data) {
    const error = new Error('AniList returned no data for this query');
    error.code = 502;
    throw error;
  }

  if (cache.size > 240) cache.clear();
  // Genuinely empty pages cache briefly only â€” a provider hiccup must never
  // stick as "zero results" for the full TTL. Failures are never cached.
  const ttl = payload.data?.Page?.media?.length ? TTL : 30 * 1000;
  cache.set(key, { value: payload.data, expires: Date.now() + ttl });
  return payload.data;
}

// Bounded retries with exponential backoff for rate limits, server errors and
// network hiccups; invalid queries and provider rejections fail fast.
async function gql(query, variables) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= RETRY_DELAYS.length; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      return await gqlOnce(query, variables);
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      const retryable = Number(error.code) === 429 || Number(error.code) >= 500;
      if (attempt === RETRY_DELAYS.length || !retryable) break;
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, RETRY_DELAYS[attempt]));
    }
  }
  throw lastError;
}

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const action = String(body.action || 'trending');
    const page = clampPage(body.page, 1, 60);
    const perPage = clampPerPage(body.perPage, 20, 25);

    if (action === 'by_id') {
      const id = Number.parseInt(body.id, 10);
      if (!Number.isFinite(id) || id <= 0) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A numeric anime id is required.' }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const data = await gql(DETAIL_QUERY, { id });
      if (!data?.Media) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'Anime not found.' }, { status: 404 });
      const media = data.Media;
      return Response.json({
        ok: true,
        item: {
          ...normalizeAniList(media),
          characters: (media.characters?.nodes || []).map((node) => ({
            id: node.id,
            name: node.name?.full || '',
            image: node.image?.large || '',
          })),
          relations: (media.relations?.edges || [])
            .filter((edge) => edge.node?.type === 'ANIME')
            .slice(0, 8)
            .map((edge) => normalizeAniList({ ...edge.node, description: '' })),
          recommendations: (media.recommendations?.nodes || [])
            .map((node) => node.mediaRecommendation)
            .filter(Boolean)
            .map((item) => normalizeAniList(item)),
        },
      });
    }

    let sort = ['POPULARITY_DESC'];
    const variables = { page, perPage, search: null, genre: null, season: null, seasonYear: null, sort };

    if (action === 'trending') sort = ['TRENDING_DESC'];
    else if (action === 'popular') sort = ['POPULARITY_DESC'];
    else if (action === 'top') sort = ['SCORE_DESC'];
    else if (action === 'seasonal') {
      const now = currentSeason();
      variables.season = (body.season || now.season).toUpperCase();
      variables.seasonYear = Number.parseInt(body.year, 10) || now.year;
    } else if (action === 'genre') {
      if (!body.genre) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A genre is required.' }, { status: 400 });
      variables.genre = String(body.genre).slice(0, 40);
    } else if (action === 'search') {
      const search = String(body.q || '').trim().slice(0, 80);
      if (!search) return Response.json({ ok: true, items: [], pageInfo: null });
      variables.search = search;
      sort = ['SEARCH_MATCH'];
      if (body.genre) variables.genre = String(body.genre).slice(0, 40);
    } else if (action !== 'browse') {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: `Unknown action "${action}".` }, { status: 400 });
    }

    variables.sort = sort;
    const data = await gql(LIST_QUERY, variables);
    const items = (data?.Page?.media || []).map(normalizeAniList).filter(Boolean);

    return Response.json({ ok: true, items, pageInfo: data?.Page?.pageInfo || null });
  } catch (error) {
    const status = Number.isFinite(error.code) && error.code >= 400 ? error.code : 502;
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: error.message || 'AniList request failed.' }, { status });
  }
}

import { secrets } from 'base44:runtime';

function readSecret(name) {
  try {
    const value = secrets.get(name);
    return value ? String(value).trim() : '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

// Readiness endpoint: reports which external services are connected WITHOUT
// ever returning a credential value.
export default async function () {
  const started = Date.now();
  const checks = {};

  try {
    const response = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query: 'query { Page(page: 1, perPage: 1) { media(type: ANIME) { id } } }' }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
    });
    checks.anilist = {
      ok: response.ok,
      status: response.status,
      label: 'AniList GraphQL',
      error: response.ok
        ? null
        : response.status === 403
          ? 'This server IP is blocked by AniList â€” the app falls back to querying AniList directly from the browser.'
          : `AniList responded ${response.status}.`,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    checks.anilist = { ok: false, status: 0, label: 'AniList GraphQL', error: error.message };
  }

  checks.ai = {
    ok: true,
    status: 200,
    label: 'PRI-ANIME AI atelier (built-in)',
    provider: 'base44',
  };

  const malRapid = readSecret('MAL_RAPIDAPI_KEY');
  const malOauth = Boolean(readSecret('MAL_CLIENT_ID')) || Boolean(readSecret('MAL_ACCESS_TOKEN'));
  const malReady = Boolean(malRapid) || malOauth;
  checks.myAnimeList = {
    ok: malReady,
    label: 'MyAnimeList API',
    status: malReady ? 200 : 0,
    provider: malRapid ? 'rapidapi' : malOauth ? 'oauth' : null,
    error: malReady ? null : 'MAL_RAPIDAPI_KEY (or MAL_CLIENT_ID) not set',
  };

  const videoProvider = readSecret('VIDEO_PROVIDER');
  const videoKey = readSecret('VIDEO_API_KEY');
  const videoUrl = readSecret('VIDEO_API_URL');
  checks.videoProvider = {
    ok: Boolean(videoProvider && videoKey && videoUrl),
    label: 'Video provider',
    provider: videoProvider || null,
    error: videoProvider && videoKey && videoUrl ? null : 'VIDEO_PROVIDER / VIDEO_API_URL / VIDEO_API_KEY not all set',
  };

  const failed = Object.values(checks).filter((check) => !check.ok).map((check) => check.label);

  return Response.json({
    ok: failed.length === 0,
    status: failed.length === 0 ? 'ready' : 'degraded',
    durationMs: Date.now() - started,
    environment: 'production',
    checks,
    note: 'Metadata is public; personalisation is stored on the device because this app runs without sign-in.',
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: base44/functions/mal/entry.ts
================================================================================
import { secrets } from 'base44:runtime';
import { normalizeMal } from '../../shared/media.ts';

const TOKEN_URL = 'https://myanimelist.net/v1/oauth2/token';
const API_BASE = 'https://api.myanimelist.net/v2';
const JIKAN_BASE = 'https://api.jikan.moe/v4';
const FIELDS = 'id,title,main_picture,alternative_titles,start_date,end_date,synopsis,mean,rank,popularity,num_list_users,num_scoring_users,nsfw,genres,media_type,status,num_episodes,start_season,broadcast,average_episode_duration,rating,pictures,background,related_anime,recommendations';

let cachedToken = null;
const cache = new Map();
const CACHE_TTL = 30 * 60 * 1000;

function readSecret(name) {
  try {
    const value = secrets.get(name);
    return value ? String(value).trim() : '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

function config() {
  return {
    rapidKey: readSecret('MAL_RAPIDAPI_KEY'),
    rapidHost: readSecret('MAL_RAPIDAPI_HOST'),
    clientId: readSecret('MAL_CLIENT_ID'),
    clientSecret: readSecret('MAL_CLIENT_SECRET'),
    accessToken: readSecret('MAL_ACCESS_TOKEN'),
    refreshToken: readSecret('MAL_REFRESH_TOKEN'),
  };
}

function oauthReady(cfg) {
  return Boolean(cfg.clientId && (cfg.accessToken || (cfg.refreshToken && cfg.clientSecret)));
}

function cached(key) {
  const hit = cache.get(key);
  if (hit && hit.expires > Date.now()) return hit.value;
  cache.delete(key);
  return null;
}

function remember(key, value) {
  if (cache.size > 300) cache.clear();
  cache.set(key, { value, expires: Date.now() + CACHE_TTL });
  return value;
}

/** RapidAPI listings wrap records in varying envelopes â€” unwrap them all. */
function entriesFrom(payload) {
  if (!payload) return [];
  if (Array.isArray(payload)) return payload;
  const list = payload.data || payload.results || payload.anime || payload.items;
  if (Array.isArray(list)) return list.map((entry) => entry?.node ?? entry);
  return [];
}

function firstRecord(payload) {
  if (!payload) return null;
  if (Array.isArray(payload)) return payload[0] ?? null;
  if (Array.isArray(payload.data)) return payload.data[0]?.node ?? payload.data[0] ?? null;
  if (payload.data && typeof payload.data === 'object') return payload.data;
  if (payload.node) return payload.node;
  if (Array.isArray(payload.results)) return payload.results[0] ?? null;
  if (payload.id || payload.mal_id || payload.title) return payload;
  return null;
}

async function getJson(url, headers = {}, timeout = 12000) {
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
  const text = await response.text();
  let payload = null;
  try {
    payload = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
  } catch {
    payload = null;
  }
  return { status: response.status, ok: response.ok, payload, text };
}

/* ---------------- RapidAPI (keyed) ---------------- */

// A keyed host that answers 404/403 for every path is misconfigured; stop
// paying for it after the first miss in this instance.
let rapidUnavailable = false;

async function rapidSearch(cfg, query, limit) {
  if (rapidUnavailable) return null;
  const encoded = encodeURIComponent(query);
  const result = await getJson(
    `https://${cfg.rapidHost}/anime?q=${encoded}&limit=${limit}&nsfw=false`,
    { 'X-RapidAPI-Key': cfg.rapidKey, 'X-RapidAPI-Host': cfg.rapidHost, Accept: 'application/json' },
    3500,
  ).catch(() => null);
  if (!result) {
    rapidUnavailable = true;
    return null;
  }
  if (!result.ok) {
    if (result.status !== 429) rapidUnavailable = true;
    return null;
  }
  const items = entriesFrom(result.payload).map(normalizeMal).filter(Boolean);
  return items.length ? items : null;
}

async function rapidItem(cfg, id) {
  if (rapidUnavailable) return null;
  const result = await getJson(
    `https://${cfg.rapidHost}/anime/${id}`,
    { 'X-RapidAPI-Key': cfg.rapidKey, 'X-RapidAPI-Host': cfg.rapidHost, Accept: 'application/json' },
    3500,
  ).catch(() => null);
  if (!result || !result.ok) {
    if (result && result.status !== 429) rapidUnavailable = true;
    return null;
  }
  const record = firstRecord(result.payload);
  return record ? normalizeMal(record) : null;
}

/* ---------------- Jikan (key-free MyAnimeList data) ---------------- */

async function jikanSearch(query, limit) {
  const result = await getJson(
    `${JIKAN_BASE}/anime?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=${limit}&sfw=true&order_by=members&sort=desc`,
  );
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`MyAnimeList lookup failed (${result.status}).`);
  return entriesFrom(result.payload).map(normalizeMal).filter(Boolean);
}

async function jikanItem(id) {
  const result = await getJson(`${JIKAN_BASE}/anime/${id}/full`);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`MyAnimeList lookup failed (${result.status}).`);
  const record = firstRecord(result.payload);
  return record ? normalizeMal(record) : null;
}

/* ---------------- Official MAL OAuth ---------------- */

async function resolveToken(cfg) {
  if (cachedToken && cachedToken.expires > Date.now()) return cachedToken.value;
  if (cfg.accessToken) return cfg.accessToken;
  if (!cfg.clientId || !cfg.clientSecret || !cfg.refreshToken) return '';

  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: cfg.clientId,
    client_secret: cfg.clientSecret,
    grant_type: 'refresh_token',
    refresh_token: cfg.refreshToken,
  });

  const response = await fetch(TOKEN_URL, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body,
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`MyAnimeList token refresh failed (${response.status}).`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  cachedToken = { value: payload.access_token, expires: Date.now() + Math.max(60, (payload.expires_in || 3600) - 60) * 1000 };
  return cachedToken.value;
}

async function oauthSearch(cfg, query, limit) {
  const token = await resolveToken(cfg);
  if (!token) throw new Error('No usable MyAnimeList access token.');
  const result = await getJson(
    `${API_BASE}/anime?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=${limit}&nsfw=false&fields=${FIELDS}`,
    { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
  );
  if (result.status === 401) {
    cachedToken = null;
    throw new Error('MyAnimeList rejected the credentials.');
  }
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`MyAnimeList returned ${result.status}.`);
  return (result.payload?.data || []).map((entry) => normalizeMal(entry.node)).filter(Boolean);
}

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const action = String(body.action || 'status');
    const cfg = config();

    const rapidReady = Boolean(cfg.rapidKey && cfg.rapidHost);
    const mode = rapidReady ? 'rapidapi' : oauthReady(cfg) ? 'oauth' : 'jikan';

    if (action === 'status') {
      return Response.json({
        ok: true,
        configured: true,
        mode,
        source: mode === 'jikan' ? 'MyAnimeList data via Jikan (key-free)' : mode === 'rapidapi' ? `RapidAPI ${cfg.rapidHost}` : 'Official MyAnimeList OAuth',
        reason: 'MyAnimeList ratings are live.',
      });
    }

    if (action === 'by_id') {
      const id = Number.parseInt(body.id, 10);
      if (!Number.isFinite(id) || id <= 0) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A numeric MAL id is required.' }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const key = `id:${id}`;
      const hit = cached(key);
      if (hit) return Response.json({ ok: true, item: hit, mode });

      const item = (rapidReady ? await rapidItem(cfg, id).catch(() => null) : null) || (await jikanItem(id));
      if (!item) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'MyAnimeList has no record for that id.' });
      return Response.json({ ok: true, item: remember(key, item), mode });
    }

    const query = String(body.q || '').trim().slice(0, 80);
    if (!query) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A search query is required.' }, { status: 400 });
    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(Number.parseInt(body.limit, 10) || 10, 1), 20);

    const key = `q:${query.toLowerCase()}:${limit}`;
    const hit = cached(key);
    if (hit) return Response.json({ ok: true, items: hit, mode });

    let items = rapidReady ? await rapidSearch(cfg, query, limit).catch(() => null) : null;
    if (!items || !items.length) {
      items = await jikanSearch(query, limit);
    }
    if (!items.length) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'MyAnimeList has no matches for that title.', mode });
    return Response.json({ ok: true, items: remember(key, items), mode });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: error.message || 'MyAnimeList request failed.' }, { status: 502 });
  }
}

FILE: base44/functions/videoJob/entry.ts

import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.54';

const STYLE_PROMPT = {
  cinematic: 'cinematic anime film still, volumetric light, shallow depth of field, film grain',
  manhwa: 'korean manhwa illustration style, bold linework, dramatic cel shading',
  painterly: 'painterly fantasy concept art, visible brushwork, mythic atmosphere',
  cel: '90s cel animation aesthetic, soft paper texture, hand-painted backgrounds',
};

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));

    const prompt = String(body.prompt || '').slice(0, 1500).trim();
    if (prompt.length < 12) {
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'Describe the shot in at least a sentence.' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const aspect = body.aspect_ratio === '9:16' ? '9:16' : '16:9';
    const styleKey = STYLE_PROMPT[body.style] ? body.style : 'cinematic';
    const ownerRef = String(body.owner_ref || 'anonymous').slice(0, 80);

    const job = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.VideoJob.create({
      owner_ref: ownerRef,
      prompt,
      language: String(body.language || 'en').slice(0, 5),
      style: styleKey,
      aspect_ratio: aspect,
      voice: String(body.voice || 'none').slice(0, 20),
      provider: 'base44:generate_video',
      status: 'processing',
      duration: 6,
    });

    const fullPrompt = `${STYLE_PROMPT[styleKey]}. ${prompt}. Original characters and world only, no existing franchise. No on-screen text, no watermark, no user interface. Vertical short-form composition.`;

    try {
      const result = await base44.asServiceRole.integrations.Core.GenerateVideo({
        prompt: fullPrompt,
        duration: 6,
        aspect_ratio: aspect,
        generate_audio: false,
      });

      const url = typeof result === 'string' ? result : result?.url || '';
      if (!url) throw new Error('The generator returned no video URL.');

      const updated = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.VideoJob.update(job.id, {
        status: 'completed',
        video_url: url,
        error: '',
      });
      return Response.json({ ok: true, job: updated });
    } catch (generationError) {
      const message = String(generationError.message || 'Video generation failed.').slice(0, 400);
      const failed = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.VideoJob.update(job.id, {
        status: 'failed',
        error: message,
      });
      return Response.json({ ok: false, error: message, job: failed, code: 'generator_unavailable' });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: error.message || 'Could not start the render job.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: base44/functions/videoProvider/entry.ts
================================================================================
import { secrets } from 'base44:runtime';
import { resolveAnimeStream } from '../../shared/animeStream.ts';

function readSecret(name) {
  try {
    const value = secrets.get(name);
    return value ? String(value).trim() : '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

const PROVIDERS = {
  cloudflare: { label: 'Cloudflare Stream', auth: 'bearer' },
  mux: { label: 'Mux', auth: 'basic' },
  generic: { label: 'Generic HLS', auth: 'bearer' },
};

export default async function (req) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const action = String(body.action || 'status');

    if (action === 'resolveAnime') {
      const animeId = Number(body.animeId);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(animeId) || animeId <= 0 || animeId > 100000000) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A valid AniList anime ID is required.' }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const match = await resolveAnimeStream(animeId);
      return Response.json({ ok: true, match });
    }

    const providerKey = readSecret('VIDEO_PROVIDER').toLowerCase();
    const apiUrl = readSecret('VIDEO_API_URL');
    const apiKey = readSecret('VIDEO_API_KEY');
    const playbackUrl = readSecret('VIDEO_PLAYBACK_URL');

    const provider = PROVIDERS[providerKey] ? providerKey : '';
    const missing = [];
    if (!provider) missing.push('VIDEO_PROVIDER');
    if (!apiUrl) missing.push('VIDEO_API_URL');
    if (!apiKey) missing.push('VIDEO_API_KEY');

    const configured = missing.length === 0;
    const status = {
      ok: true,
      configured,
      provider: provider || null,
      providerLabel: provider ? PROVIDERS[provider].label : null,
      playbackBaseConfigured: Boolean(playbackUrl),
      missing,
      reason: configured
        ? `Server-side ${PROVIDERS[provider].label} credentials detected.`
        : 'No video provider connected. Playback falls back to the video URLs stored on each record.',
    };

    if (action === 'status') return Response.json(status);

    if (action === 'playback') {
      const videoId = String(body.videoId || '').trim().slice(0, 128);
      if (!videoId) return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'A videoId is required.' }, { status: 400 });
      if (!configured) return Response.json({ ...status, ok: false, error: 'Video provider not configured.' });

      const headers = {
        Authorization:
          PROVIDERS[provider].auth === 'basic'
            ? `Basic ${btoa(apiKey)}`
            : `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
      };

      let response;
      try {
        response = await fetch(`${apiUrl.replace(/\/$/, '')}/${encodeURIComponent(videoId)}`, {
          headers,
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000),
        });
      } catch (networkError) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, configured: true, error: `Provider unreachable: ${networkError.message}` }, { status: 502 });
      }

      if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, configured: true, error: 'Provider rejected the credentials.' }, { status: 401 });
      }
      if (response.status === 404) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, configured: true, error: 'Video not found at the provider.' }, { status: 404 });
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, configured: true, error: `Provider returned ${response.status}.` }, { status: 502 });
      }

      const payload = await response.json();

      if (provider === 'cloudflare') {
        const playback = payload?.result?.playback || {};
        return Response.json({
          ok: true,
          provider,
          hls: playback.hls || '',
          dash: playback.dash || '',
          thumbnail: payload?.result?.thumbnail || '',
          ready: payload?.result?.status?.state === 'ready',
        });
      }
      if (provider === 'mux') {
        const playbackId = payload?.data?.playback_ids?.[0]?.id || '';
        return Response.json({
          ok: true,
          provider,
          hls: playbackId ? `${(playbackUrl || 'https://stream.mux.com').replace(/\/$/, '')}/${playbackId}.m3u8` : '',
          thumbnail: payload?.data?.playback_ids?.[0]?.id ? `${(playbackUrl || 'https://image.mux.com').replace(/\/$/, '')}/${playbackId}/thumbnail.jpg` : '',
          ready: payload?.data?.status === 'ready',
        });
      }

      return Response.json({ ok: true, provider, raw: { hls: payload?.hls || '', ready: Boolean(payload) } });
    }

    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: `Unknown action "${action}".` }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ ok: false, error: error.message || 'Provider check failed.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: base44/shared/animeStream.ts
================================================================================
const MAPPING_URL = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Fribb/anime-lists/master/anime-list-full.json';
const cache = new Map();
const pending = new Map();

// Verified against https://www.themoviedb.org/tv/280564-false-memory.
// This recent title is not yet mapped in the community registry.
const verified = {
  141953: { mediaType: 'tv', tmdbId: 280564, season: 1, episodeOffset: 0, source: 'TMDB verified match' },
};

async function findRecord(animeId) {
  const response = await fetch(MAPPING_URL, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000) });
  if (!response.ok || !response.body) throw new Error('The anime ID registry is temporarily unavailable.');
  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let depth = 0;
  let quoted = false;
  let escaped = false;
  let record = '';
  try {
    while (true) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done) return null;
      for (const char of decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })) {
        if (depth === 0) {
          if (char === '{') { depth = 1; record = '{'; }
          continue;
        }
        record += char;
        if (quoted) {
          if (escaped) escaped = false;
          else if (char === '\\') escaped = true;
          else if (char === '"') quoted = false;
          continue;
        }
        if (char === '"') quoted = true;
        else if (char === '{') depth += 1;
        else if (char === '}') depth -= 1;
        if (depth === 0) {
          const row = JSON.parse(record);
          record = '';
          if (Number(row.anilist_id) === animeId) {
            await reader.cancel();
            return row;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
}

function normalizeRecord(row) {
  if (!row) return null;
  const movies = row.themoviedb_id?.movie || [];
  const mediaType = row.type === 'MOVIE' && movies.length === 1 ? 'movie' : row.themoviedb_id?.tv ? 'tv' : null;
  const tmdbId = Number(mediaType === 'movie' ? movies[0] : row.themoviedb_id?.tv);
  if (!mediaType || !Number.isSafeInteger(tmdbId) || tmdbId <= 0) return null;
  const season = Number(row.season?.tmdb ?? 1);
  const episodeOffset = Number(row.episode_offset?.tmdb ?? 0);
  if (!Number.isInteger(season) || season < 0 || !Number.isInteger(episodeOffset)) return null;
  return { mediaType, tmdbId, season, episodeOffset, source: 'Fribb anime-lists', seasonVerified: row.season?.tmdb != null };
}

export async function resolveAnimeStream(animeId) {
  const decorate = (match) => match ? { ...match, tmdbUrl: `https://www.themoviedb.org/${match.mediaType}/${match.tmdbId}` } : null;
  if (verified[animeId]) return decorate(verified[animeId]);
  const hit = cache.get(animeId);
  if (hit && hit.expires > Date.now()) return hit.value;
  if (pending.has(animeId)) return pending.get(animeId);
  const lookup = findRecord(animeId).then((row) => {
    const value = decorate(normalizeRecord(row));
    if (cache.size >= 500) cache.clear();
    cache.set(animeId, { value, expires: Date.now() + (value ? 43200000 : 3600000) });
    return value;
  }).finally(() => pending.delete(animeId));
  pending.set(animeId, lookup);
  return lookup;
}

FILE: base44/shared/media.ts

/**
 * Shared normalisation for metadata providers (AniList + MyAnimeList).
 * Both providers are metadata only â€” neither serves episode streams.
 */

export function normalizeAniList(media) {
  if (!media) return null;
  return {
    id: media.id,
    source: "anilist",
    title: {
      romaji: media.title?.romaji || "",
      english: media.title?.english || media.title?.romaji || "",
      native: media.title?.native || "",
    },
    display: media.title?.english || media.title?.romaji || "Untitled",
    cover: media.coverImage?.extraLarge || media.coverImage?.large || "",
    color: media.coverImage?.color || "",
    banner: media.bannerImage || media.coverImage?.extraLarge || "",
    description: (media.description || "").replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, " ").replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ""),
    genres: media.genres || [],
    score: media.averageScore ?? null,
    meanScore: media.meanScore ?? null,
    popularity: media.popularity ?? 0,
    episodes: media.episodes ?? null,
    duration: media.duration ?? null,
    status: media.status || "",
    format: media.format || "",
    season: media.season || "",
    seasonYear: media.seasonYear ?? null,
    startDate: media.startDate || null,
    studios: (media.studios?.nodes || []).map((node) => node.name),
    trailer: media.trailer || null,
    nextEpisode: media.nextAiringEpisode || null,
  };
}

export function normalizeMal(anime) {
  if (!anime) return null;
  // Tolerates the official v2 shape, Jikan/RapidAPI variants and plain records.
  const mean = Number(
    anime.mean ?? anime.score ?? anime.rating ?? Number.NaN,
  );
  const cover =
    anime.main_picture?.large ||
    anime.main_picture?.medium ||
    anime.images?.jpg?.large_image_url ||
    anime.images?.jpg?.image_url ||
    anime.picture ||
    "";
  return {
    id: anime.id ?? anime.mal_id ?? null,
    source: "mal",
    display: anime.title || anime.name || "Untitled",
    title: {
      romaji: anime.alternative_titles?.en || anime.title || anime.name || "",
      english: anime.alternative_titles?.en || anime.title || anime.name || "",
      native: anime.alternative_titles?.ja || anime.title_japanese || "",
    },
    cover,
    description: anime.synopsis || anime.description || "",
    genres: (anime.genres || []).map((genre) => (typeof genre === "string" ? genre : genre.name)).filter(Boolean),
    score: Number.isFinite(mean) && mean > 0 ? Math.round(mean * 10) : null,
    malScore: Number.isFinite(mean) && mean > 0 ? mean : null,
    rank: anime.rank ?? null,
    popularity: anime.popularity ?? anime.members ?? 0,
    episodes: anime.num_episodes ?? anime.episodes ?? null,
    status: anime.status || "",
    format: anime.media_type || anime.type || "",
    seasonYear: anime.start_season?.year ?? anime.year ?? null,
    season: anime.start_season?.season ?? anime.season ?? "",
  };
}

export function currentSeason(date = new Date()) {
  const month = date.getUTCMonth();
  const season = month < 3 ? "WINTER" : month < 6 ? "SPRING" : month < 9 ? "SUMMER" : "FALL";
  return { season, year: date.getUTCFullYear() };
}

export function clampPage(value, fallback = 1, max = 200) {
  const page = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(page) || page < 1) return fallback;
  return Math.min(page, max);
}

export function clampPerPage(value, fallback = 20, max = 25) {
  const perPage = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(perPage) || perPage < 1) return fallback;
  return Math.min(perPage, max);
}

import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster"
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { queryClientInstance } from '@/lib/query-client'
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import PageNotFound from './lib/PageNotFound';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';
import ScrollToTop from './components/ScrollToTop';
import { ThemeProvider } from '@/lib/theme';
import Layout from '@/components/layout/Layout';
// Add page imports here
import Home from '@/pages/Home';
import Browse from '@/pages/Browse';
import Category from '@/pages/Category';
import AnimeDetail from '@/pages/AnimeDetail';
import Reels from '@/pages/Reels';
import Videos from '@/pages/Videos';
import Watch from '@/pages/Watch';
import Studio from '@/pages/Studio';
import Creator from '@/pages/Creator';
import Library from '@/pages/Library';
import Settings from '@/pages/Settings';
import Admin from '@/pages/Admin';
import Policy from '@/pages/Policy';

const AuthenticatedApp = () => {
  const { isLoadingAuth, isLoadingPublicSettings, authError, navigateToLogin } = useAuth();

  // Show loading spinner while checking app public settings or auth
  if (isLoadingPublicSettings || isLoadingAuth) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Handle authentication errors
  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    } else if (authError.type === 'auth_required') {
      // Redirect to login automatically
      navigateToLogin();
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Render the main app
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
        <Route path="/browse" element={<Browse />} />
        <Route path="/category/:slug" element={<Category />} />
        <Route path="/anime/:id" element={<AnimeDetail />} />
        <Route path="/reels" element={<Reels />} />
        <Route path="/watch" element={<Videos />} />
        <Route path="/watch/:id" element={<Watch />} />
        <Route path="/studio" element={<Studio />} />
        <Route path="/creator" element={<Creator />} />
        <Route path="/library" element={<Library />} />
        <Route path="/settings" element={<Settings />} />
        <Route path="/admin" element={<Admin />} />
        <Route path="/policy" element={<Policy />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="*" element={<PageNotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  );
};


function App() {

  return (
    <ThemeProvider>
      <AuthProvider>
        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClientInstance}>
          <Router>
            <ScrollToTop />
            <AuthenticatedApp />
          </Router>
          <Toaster />
        </QueryClientProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  )
}

export default App

          {children}
        </div>

          <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-6">{footer}</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";

export default function GoogleIcon({ className = "w-5 h-5" }) {
  return (
    <svg className={className} viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92a5.06 5.06 0 01-2.2 3.32v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.1z" fill="#4285F4" />
      <path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853" />
      <path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z" fill="#FBBC05" />
      <path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z" fill="#EA4335" />
    </svg>
  );

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';

const DefaultFallback = () => (
  <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
    <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
  </div>
);

export default function ProtectedRoute({ fallback = <DefaultFallback />, unauthenticatedElement }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, isLoadingAuth, authChecked, authError, checkUserAuth } = useAuth();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authChecked && !isLoadingAuth) {
      checkUserAuth();
    }
  }, [authChecked, isLoadingAuth, checkUserAuth]);

  if (isLoadingAuth || !authChecked) {
    return fallback;
  }

  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    }
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ScrollToTop.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigationType } from "react-router-dom";

const getHashId = (hash) => {
  const rawId = hash.slice(1);

  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(rawId);
  } catch {
    return rawId;
  }
};

export default function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
  const navigationType = useNavigationType();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (navigationType === "POP") return;

    if (hash) {
      const id = getHashId(hash);
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
      }, 50);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  }, [pathname, hash, navigationType]);

  return null;
}

FILE: src/components/anime/AnimeCard.jsx

import { useRef } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Star, Play } from "lucide-react";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";
import { formatCount } from "@/lib/format";
import { useHoverPreview } from "@/hooks/useHoverPreview";
import HoverPreviewPortal from "@/components/anime/HoverPreviewPortal";
import AnimeHoverPreview from "@/components/anime/AnimeHoverPreview";

const finePointer =
  typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia?.("(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)").matches;

export default function AnimeCard({ anime, rank, className = "" }) {
  const cardRef = useRef(null);
  const preview = useHoverPreview();
  if (!anime) return null;

  const hoverHandlers = finePointer
    ? {
        onMouseEnter: preview.openPreview,
        onMouseLeave: preview.scheduleClose,
        onFocus: preview.openPreview,
        onBlur: preview.scheduleClose,
      }
    : {};

  return (
    <>
      <Link
        ref={cardRef}
        to={`/anime/${anime.id}`}
        className={`group relative block overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card/60 glow-hover ${className}`}
        aria-label={anime.display}
        {...hoverHandlers}
      >
        <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] overflow-hidden bg-muted">
          {anime.cover ? (
            <Image
              src={anime.cover}
              alt=""
              loading="lazy"
              className="h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-700 group-hover:scale-[1.06]"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="h-full w-full bg-gradient-to-br from-secondary to-background" />
          )}

          <div
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/20 to-transparent opacity-90"
          />

          {typeof rank === "number" && (
            <span className="absolute left-2 top-2 rounded-md bg-black/70 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-display text-sm text-primary backdrop-blur">
              #{rank}
            </span>
          )}

          {anime.score ? (
            <span className="absolute right-2 top-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-black/70 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-white backdrop-blur">
              <Star className="h-2.5 w-2.5 fill-primary text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
              {(anime.score / 10).toFixed(1)}
            </span>
          ) : null}

          <span className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center opacity-0 transition group-hover:opacity-100">
            <span className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full border border-primary/70 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur">
              <Play className="h-4 w-4 fill-primary text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
          </span>

          <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 p-2.5">
            <p className="line-clamp-2 font-display text-[15px] leading-tight text-white">{anime.display}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-white/60">
              {[anime.format?.replace(/_/g, " "), anime.seasonYear, anime.episodes ? `${anime.episodes} ep` : null]
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(" Â· ")}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-xl opacity-0 transition duration-500 group-hover:opacity-100"
          style={{ boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px hsl(var(--primary) / 0.4), 0 0 44px -6px hsl(var(--primary) / 0.35)" }}
        />
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-1/2 top-0 h-full w-1/2 -skew-x-12 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-primary/10 to-transparent opacity-0 blur-md transition-all duration-700 group-hover:left-full group-hover:opacity-100"
        />

        <span className="sr-only">
          {anime.popularity ? `${formatCount(anime.popularity)} followers` : ""}
        </span>
      </Link>

      {finePointer && (
        <HoverPreviewPortal
          anchorRef={cardRef}
          open={preview.open}
          onPointerEnter={preview.cancelClose}
          onPointerLeave={preview.scheduleClose}
        >
          <AnimeHoverPreview anime={anime} />
        </HoverPreviewPortal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/anime/AnimeHoverPreview.jsx
================================================================================
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { Play, BookmarkPlus, BookmarkCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";
import { useWatchlist } from "@/hooks/useWatchlist";
import { seasonLabel } from "@/lib/format";

const EASE = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1];

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  RELEASING: "Ongoing",
  FINISHED: "Completed",
  NOT_YET_RELEASED: "Upcoming",
  CANCELLED: "Cancelled",
  HIATUS: "On hiatus",
};

const stripTags = (text) => (text || "").replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();

export default function AnimeHoverPreview({ anime }) {
  const watchlist = useWatchlist("watchlist");
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const saved = watchlist.isSaved(anime.id);
  const statusLabel = STATUS_LABELS[anime.status] || (anime.status ? anime.status.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, " ") : "");

  const chips = [
    anime.score ? `${(anime.score / 10).toFixed(1)} rating` : null,
    anime.seasonYear ? String(anime.seasonYear) : null,
    anime.episodes ? `${anime.episodes} episodes` : null,
    anime.season ? seasonLabel(anime.season, null) : null,
    statusLabel || null,
  ].filter(Boolean);

  const altTitle = anime.titleRomaji && anime.titleRomaji !== anime.display ? anime.titleRomaji : null;

  const stagger = (index) => ({
    initial: { opacity: 0, y: 6 },
    animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 },
    transition: { duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.3, delay: reduced ? 0 : 0.05 + index * 0.05, ease: EASE },
  });

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-label={`${anime.display} preview`}
      className="glass glow-soft overflow-hidden rounded-2xl shadow-2xl"
      style={{ borderColor: "hsl(var(--primary) / 0.35)", boxShadow: "0 0 46px -14px hsl(var(--primary) / 0.45), 0 24px 70px -24px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.7)" }}
    >
      <div className="relative h-32 overflow-hidden">
        {anime.banner || anime.cover ? (
          <Image src={anime.banner || anime.cover} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
        ) : (
          <div className="h-full w-full bg-gradient-to-br from-secondary to-background" />
        )}
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute inset-0"
          style={{ background: "linear-gradient(to top, hsl(var(--card)) 4%, hsl(var(--card) / 0.45) 45%, transparent 100%)" }}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="px-4 pb-4">
        <motion.div {...stagger(0)} className="-mt-6 relative">
          <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-medium leading-snug text-foreground text-balance">{anime.display}</h3>
          {altTitle && <p className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{altTitle}</p>}
        </motion.div>

        {chips.length > 0 && (
          <motion.div {...stagger(1)} className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {chips.map((chip) => (
              <span
                key={chip}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/60 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground"
              >
                {chip}
              </span>
            ))}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        {anime.genres?.length > 0 && (
          <motion.div {...stagger(2)} className="mt-2.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {anime.genres.slice(0, 4).map((genre) => (
              <span key={genre} className="rounded-full bg-primary/12 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-primary">
                {genre}
              </span>
            ))}
          </motion.div>
        )}

        <motion.p {...stagger(3)} className="mt-3 line-clamp-4 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-foreground/75">
          {stripTags(anime.description) || "No synopsis has been published for this title yet."}
        </motion.p>

        <motion.div {...stagger(4)} className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link
            to={`/watch?anime=${anime.id}`}
            className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition hover:brightness-110"
          >
            <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Watch now
          </Link>
          <Link
            to={`/anime/${anime.id}`}
            className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-border/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-foreground transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
          >
            View details
          </Link>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => watchlist.toggle(anime)}
            disabled={watchlist.loading}
            aria-label={saved ? "Remove from watchlist" : "Add to watchlist"}
            className={`grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border transition ${
              saved ? "border-primary/70 bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border/80 text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
            }`}
          >
            {watchlist.loading ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : saved ? (
              <BookmarkCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            ) : (
              <BookmarkPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            )}
          </button>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/anime/AnimeRow.jsx
================================================================================
import { useRef } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import AnimeCard from "@/components/anime/AnimeCard";
import SkeletonCard from "@/components/anime/SkeletonCard";

export default function AnimeRow({ title, subtitle, items = [], loading, error, viewAll, rank }) {
  const scroller = useRef(null);

  const scroll = (direction) => {
    const node = scroller.current;
    if (!node) return;
    node.scrollBy({ left: direction * Math.max(320, node.clientWidth * 0.8), behavior: "smooth" });
  };

  return (
    <section className="py-7">
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-medium leading-tight sm:text-[27px]">{title}</h2>
          {subtitle && <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">{subtitle}</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {viewAll && (
            <Link to={viewAll} className="hidden text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary sm:block">
              View all
            </Link>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => scroll(-1)}
            aria-label={`Scroll ${title} left`}
            className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full border border-border/70 text-muted-foreground transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => scroll(1)}
            aria-label={`Scroll ${title} right`}
            className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full border border-border/70 text-muted-foreground transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error ? (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-destructive-foreground">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span className="text-foreground/80">{error}</span>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div
          ref={scroller}
          className="no-scrollbar -mx-4 flex snap-x snap-mandatory gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-1 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0"
        >
          {loading
            ? Array.from({ length: 7 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="w-[150px] shrink-0 sm:w-[165px]">
                  <SkeletonCard />
                </div>
              ))
            : items.map((anime, index) => (
                <div key={`${anime.id}-${index}`} className="w-[150px] shrink-0 snap-start sm:w-[165px]">
                  <AnimeCard anime={anime} rank={rank ? index + 1 : undefined} />
                </div>
              ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/anime/HoverPreviewPortal.jsx
================================================================================
import { useLayoutEffect, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";

const WIDTH = 344;
const MARGIN = 12;
const EST_HEIGHT = 500;
const EASE = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1];

export default function HoverPreviewPortal({ anchorRef, open, onPointerEnter, onPointerLeave, children }) {
  const [pos, setPos] = useState(null);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const compute = () => {
      const el = anchorRef.current;
      if (!el) return;
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const vw = window.innerWidth;
      const vh = window.innerHeight;
      let x;
      if (rect.right + WIDTH + MARGIN <= vw - MARGIN) {
        x = rect.right + MARGIN;
      } else if (rect.left - WIDTH - MARGIN >= MARGIN) {
        x = rect.left - WIDTH - MARGIN;
      } else {
        x = Math.min(Math.max(rect.left - WIDTH / 2, MARGIN), Math.max(MARGIN, vw - WIDTH - MARGIN));
      }
      let y = rect.top + rect.height / 2 - EST_HEIGHT / 2;
      y = Math.min(Math.max(y, MARGIN), Math.max(MARGIN, vh - EST_HEIGHT - MARGIN));
      setPos({ left: Math.round(x), top: Math.round(y) });
    };
    compute();
    window.addEventListener("resize", compute);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", compute);
  }, [open, anchorRef]);

  return createPortal(
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && pos && (
        <motion.div
          key="anime-hover-preview"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10, scale: 0.97 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 8, scale: 0.97 }}
          transition={{ duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.24, ease: EASE }}
          className="fixed z-[70]"
          style={{ left: pos.left, top: pos.top, width: WIDTH }}
          onPointerEnter={onPointerEnter}
          onPointerLeave={onPointerLeave}
        >
          {children}
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>,
    document.body
  );
}

        <Icon className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>

        </Link>

          type="button"

          className="mt-5 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition hover:brightness-110"

export default function PageHeader({ eyebrow, title, description, children }) {
  return (
    <header className="border-b border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-b from-card/50 to-transparent">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-10 lg:py-14">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-5">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            {eyebrow && (
              <p className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-primary">
                <span className="h-px w-8 bg-primary/70" aria-hidden="true" />
                {eyebrow}
              </p>
            )}
            <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-4xl font-medium leading-[1.05] tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">
              {title}
            </h1>
            {description && <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>}
          </div>
          {children && <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{children}</div>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/common/ReportDialog.jsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Flag, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const REASONS = ["Copyright / unlicensed", "Sexual content", "Violence", "Hate speech", "Spam", "Misinformed metadata"];

export default function ReportDialog({ targetType, targetId, targetLabel, trigger }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState(REASONS[0]);
  const [details, setDetails] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setStatus("saving");
    setError("");
    try {
      await base44.entities.Report.create({
        target_type: targetType,
        target_id: String(targetId),
        target_label: (targetLabel || "").slice(0, 140),
        reason,
        details: details.slice(0, 600),
        reporter_ref: getProfile().id,
        status: "open",
      });
      setStatus("done");
      setDetails("");
    } catch (submitError) {
      setStatus("idle");
      setError(submitError.message || "Could not send the report.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(next) => { setOpen(next); if (!next) setStatus("idle"); }}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        {trigger || (
          <button type="button" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary">
            <Flag className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Report
          </button>
        )}
      </DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent className="border-border/70 bg-card/95 backdrop-blur-xl sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="font-display text-2xl">Report content</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Reports go to the admin moderation queue. Tell us what is wrong with â€œ{targetLabel}â€.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        {status === "done" ? (
          <div className="py-6 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-foreground">Report received. Thank you.</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">An admin will review it in the moderation queue.</p>
            <Button variant="outline" className="mt-5" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
              Close
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="report-reason" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                Reason
              </label>
              <select
                id="report-reason"
                value={reason}
                onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                {REASONS.map((option) => (
                  <option key={option} value={option}>
                    {option}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="report-details" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                Details (optional)
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="report-details"
                value={details}
                onChange={(event) => setDetails(event.target.value)}
                rows={3}
                maxLength={600}
                className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                placeholder="Anything the moderator should know."
              />
            </div>
            {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
            <Button type="submit" disabled={status === "saving"} className="w-full">
              {status === "saving" ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
              Send report
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/effects/CursorGlow.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

export default function CursorGlow() {
  const ref = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    const fine = window.matchMedia("(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)").matches;
    if (reduced || !fine || !ref.current) return undefined;

    const el = ref.current;
    const pos = { x: window.innerWidth / 2, y: window.innerHeight / 2 };
    const target = { ...pos };
    let raf = 0;
    let running = false;

    const tick = () => {
      pos.x += (target.x - pos.x) * 0.14;
      pos.y += (target.y - pos.y) * 0.14;
      el.style.transform = `translate3d(${pos.x}px, ${pos.y}px, 0) translate(-50%, -50%)`;
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    const start = () => {
      if (running) return;
      running = true;
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    const stop = () => {
      running = false;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    };

    const onMove = (event) => {
      target.x = event.clientX;
      target.y = event.clientY;
      el.style.opacity = "1";
      start();
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      el.style.opacity = "0";
    };
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) stop();
    };

    window.addEventListener("pointermove", onMove, { passive: true });
    document.documentElement.addEventListener("pointerleave", onLeave);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);

    return () => {
      stop();
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", onMove);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener("pointerleave", onLeave);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="pointer-events-none fixed left-0 top-0 z-[35] h-[560px] w-[560px] rounded-full opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-700"
      style={{
        background:
          "radial-gradient(circle at center, hsl(var(--primary) / 0.16) 0%, hsl(var(--primary) / 0.07) 32%, transparent 65%)",
        mixBlendMode: "screen",
      }}
    />
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/effects/InteractiveSmoke.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

const MAX_PARTICLES = 130;
const SPRITE_SIZE = 160;

function makeSprite(channel) {
  const value = window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(channel).trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  const sprite = document.createElement("canvas");
  sprite.width = sprite.height = SPRITE_SIZE;
  const ctx = sprite.getContext("2d");
  const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(SPRITE_SIZE / 2, SPRITE_SIZE / 2, 0, SPRITE_SIZE / 2, SPRITE_SIZE / 2, SPRITE_SIZE / 2);
  gradient.addColorStop(0, `hsl(${value} / 0.5)`);
  gradient.addColorStop(0.45, `hsl(${value} / 0.2)`);
  gradient.addColorStop(1, `hsl(${value} / 0)`);
  ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, SPRITE_SIZE, SPRITE_SIZE);
  return sprite;
}

export default function InteractiveSmoke() {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    const fine = window.matchMedia("(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)").matches;
    if (reduced || !fine || !canvasRef.current) return undefined;

    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const purple = makeSprite("--primary");
    if (!purple) return undefined;
    const violet = makeSprite("--chart-2");
    const gold = makeSprite("--gold");
    const sprites = [purple, purple, violet || purple];
    if (gold) sprites.push(gold);
    const goldIndex = gold ? sprites.length - 1 : -1;

    let dpr = 1;
    let width = 0;
    let height = 0;
    const resize = () => {
      dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      width = window.innerWidth;
      height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.width = Math.round(width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.round(height * dpr);
      canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;
      canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };
    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);

    const pool = Array.from({ length: MAX_PARTICLES }, () => ({
      active: false, x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, r: 0, grow: 0,
      life: 0, maxLife: 0, phase: 0, spin: 0, sprite: 0,
    }));
    let poolIndex = 0;

    const pointer = { x: width / 2, y: height / 2, seen: false };
    const emitter = { x: pointer.x, y: pointer.y };
    let spawnCarry = 0;
    let running = false;
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;

    const spawn = (x, y) => {
      const p = pool[poolIndex];
      poolIndex = (poolIndex + 1) % MAX_PARTICLES;
      p.active = true;
      p.x = x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
      p.y = y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
      p.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 12;
      p.vy = -6 - Math.random() * 14;
      p.r = 14 + Math.random() * 18;
      p.grow = 14 + Math.random() * 20;
      p.maxLife = 1.5 + Math.random() * 1.1;
      p.life = 0;
      p.phase = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      p.spin = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.4;
      p.sprite = goldIndex >= 0 && Math.random() < 0.07 ? goldIndex : Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
    };

    const tick = (now) => {
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000 || 0.016);
      last = now;

      const dx = pointer.x - emitter.x;
      const dy = pointer.y - emitter.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (dist > 1) {
        const step = Math.min(dist, Math.max(6, dist * 0.28));
        emitter.x += (dx / dist) * step;
        emitter.y += (dy / dist) * step;
        spawnCarry += step;
        while (spawnCarry >= 26) {
          spawnCarry -= 26;
          spawn(emitter.x, emitter.y);
        }
      }

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
      for (const p of pool) {
        if (!p.active) continue;
        p.life += dt;
        if (p.life >= p.maxLife) {
          p.active = false;
          continue;
        }
        p.phase += dt * p.spin;
        p.x += (p.vx + Math.sin(p.phase * 2) * 8) * dt;
        p.y += p.vy * dt;
        p.vy -= dt * 3;
        p.r += p.grow * dt;
        const k = p.life / p.maxLife;
        const fade = k < 0.25 ? k / 0.25 : 1 - (k - 0.25) / 0.75;
        const size = p.r * 2;
        ctx.globalAlpha = fade * 0.5;
        ctx.drawImage(sprites[p.sprite], p.x - size / 2, p.y - size / 2, size, size);
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };

    const start = () => {
      if (running) return;
      running = true;
      last = performance.now();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    const stop = () => {
      running = false;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    };

    const onMove = (event) => {
      pointer.x = event.clientX;
      pointer.y = event.clientY;
      if (!pointer.seen) {
        pointer.seen = true;
        emitter.x = pointer.x;
        emitter.y = pointer.y;
      }
      start();
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      pointer.seen = false;
    };
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) stop();
      else if (pointer.seen) start();
    };

    window.addEventListener("pointermove", onMove, { passive: true });
    document.documentElement.addEventListener("pointerleave", onLeave);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);

    return () => {
      stop();
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize);
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", onMove);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener("pointerleave", onLeave);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    };
  }, []);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0 z-40" />;
}
