const sidebarMenuButtonVariants = cva(
  "peer/menu-button flex w-full items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden rounded-md p-2 text-left text-sm outline-none ring-sidebar-ring transition-[width,height,padding] hover:bg-sidebar-accent hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground focus-visible:ring-2 active:bg-sidebar-accent active:text-sidebar-accent-foreground disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 group-has-[[data-sidebar=menu-action]]/menu-item:pr-8 aria-disabled:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:opacity-50 data-[active=true]:bg-sidebar-accent data-[active=true]:font-medium data-[active=true]:text-sidebar-accent-foreground data-[state=open]:hover:bg-sidebar-accent data-[state=open]:hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground group-data-[collapsible=icon]:!size-8 group-data-[collapsible=icon]:!p-2 [&>span:last-child]:truncate [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "hover:bg-sidebar-accent hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground",
        outline:
          "bg-background shadow-[0_0_0_1px_hsl(var(--sidebar-border))] hover:bg-sidebar-accent hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground hover:shadow-[0_0_0_1px_hsl(var(--sidebar-accent))]",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-8 text-sm",
        sm: "h-7 text-xs",
        lg: "h-12 text-sm group-data-[collapsible=icon]:!p-0",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

const SidebarMenuButton = React.forwardRef((
  {
    asChild = false,
    isActive = false,
    variant = "default",
    size = "default",
    tooltip,
    className,
    ...props
  },
  ref
) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
  const { isMobile, state } = useSidebar()

  const button = (
    <Comp
      ref={ref}
      data-sidebar="menu-button"
      data-size={size}
      data-active={isActive}
      className={cn(sidebarMenuButtonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
      {...props} />
  )

  if (!tooltip) {
    return button
  }

  if (typeof tooltip === "string") {
    tooltip = {
      children: tooltip,
    }
  }

  return (
    (<Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger asChild>{button}</TooltipTrigger>
      <TooltipContent
        side="right"
        align="center"
        hidden={state !== "collapsed" || isMobile}
        {...tooltip} />
    </Tooltip>)
  );
})
SidebarMenuButton.displayName = "SidebarMenuButton"

const SidebarMenuAction = React.forwardRef(({ className, asChild = false, showOnHover = false, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"

  return (
    (<Comp
      ref={ref}
      data-sidebar="menu-action"
      className={cn(
        "absolute right-1 top-1.5 flex aspect-square w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md p-0 text-sidebar-foreground outline-none ring-sidebar-ring transition-transform hover:bg-sidebar-accent hover:text-sidebar-accent-foreground focus-visible:ring-2 peer-hover/menu-button:text-sidebar-accent-foreground [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:shrink-0",
        // Increases the hit area of the button on mobile.
        "after:absolute after:-inset-2 after:md:hidden",
        "peer-data-[size=sm]/menu-button:top-1",
        "peer-data-[size=default]/menu-button:top-1.5",
        "peer-data-[size=lg]/menu-button:top-2.5",
        "group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden",
        showOnHover &&
        "group-focus-within/menu-item:opacity-100 group-hover/menu-item:opacity-100 data-[state=open]:opacity-100 peer-data-[active=true]/menu-button:text-sidebar-accent-foreground md:opacity-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
SidebarMenuAction.displayName = "SidebarMenuAction"

    return `${Math.floor(Math.random() * 40) + 50}%`;
  }, [])

  return (
    (<div
      ref={ref}
      data-sidebar="menu-skeleton"
      className={cn("flex h-8 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2", className)}
      {...props}>
      {showIcon && (
        <Skeleton className="size-4 rounded-md" data-sidebar="menu-skeleton-icon" />
      )}
      <Skeleton
        className="h-4 max-w-[--skeleton-width] flex-1"
        data-sidebar="menu-skeleton-text"
        style={
          {
            "--skeleton-width": width
          }
        } />
    </div>)
  );
})

FILE: src/components/ui/toaster.jsx

import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import {
  Toast,
  ToastClose,
  ToastDescription,
  ToastProvider,
  ToastTitle,
  ToastViewport,
} from "@/components/ui/toast";

export function Toaster() {
  const { toasts } = useToast();

  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      {toasts.map(function ({ id, title, description, action, ...props }) {
        return (
          <Toast key={id} {...props}>
            <div className="grid gap-1">
              {title && <ToastTitle>{title}</ToastTitle>}
              {description && (
                <ToastDescription>{description}</ToastDescription>
              )}
            </div>
            {action}
            <ToastClose />
          </Toast>
        );
      })}
      <ToastViewport />
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/use-responsive-image.jsx
================================================================================
import * as React from "react"
import { useSize } from "../../hooks/use-size"
import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"
import { DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH, getImagePreviewClassName } from "./image-helpers"

export function useResponsiveImage({ parsed, fittingType, focalPoint, quality, className, onLoad, onSourceChange }, parentRef) {
  const wrapperRef = React.useRef(null)
  const imgRef = React.useRef(null)
  const size = useSize(wrapperRef)
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false)

  React.useImperativeHandle(parentRef, () => imgRef.current)
  React.useEffect(() => setLoaded(false), [parsed.baseUrl])
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const wrapper = wrapperRef.current
    const replace = (event) => onSourceChange(
      event.detail.src, getImagePreviewClassName(className, wrapper.className, cn("inline-block relative", className))
    )
    wrapper.addEventListener("base44:image-replace", replace)
    return () => wrapper.removeEventListener("base44:image-replace", replace)
  }, [className, onSourceChange])

  const crop = fittingType !== "fit"
  // Wait for useSize's pre-paint measurement before requesting a transform.
  const options = size && {
    width: size.width || DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH,
    height: size.height || undefined,
    crop,
    focalPoint: crop ? focalPoint : undefined,
    quality,
  }

  return {
    wrapperRef, imgRef, loaded, options,
    handleLoad: (event) => {
      setLoaded(true)
      onLoad?.(event)
    },
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/use-toast.jsx
================================================================================
// Inspired by react-hot-toast library
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

const TOAST_LIMIT = 20;
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
};

let count = 0;

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_VALUE;
  return count.toString();
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map();

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return;
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST,
      toastId,
    });
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);
};

const _clearFromRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  const timeout = toastTimeouts.get(toastId);
  if (timeout) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
  }
};

export const reducer = (state, action) => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case actionTypes.ADD_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      };

    case actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t
        ),
      };

    case actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST: {
      const { toastId } = action;

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);
        });
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t
        ),
      };
    }
    case actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST:
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        };
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      };
  }
};

const listeners = [];

let memoryState = { toasts: [] };

function dispatch(action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState);
  });
}

function toast({ ...props }) {
  const id = genId();

  const update = (props) =>
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST,
      toast: { ...props, id },
    });

  const dismiss = () =>
    dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId: id });

  dispatch({
    type: actionTypes.ADD_TOAST,
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss();
      },
    },
  });

  return {
    id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  };
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = useState(memoryState);

  useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState);
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    };
  }, [state]);

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId) => dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId }),
  };
}

export { useToast, toast };

import { useState } from "react";
import { Play } from "lucide-react";
import EmbedIframe from "@/components/video/EmbedIframe";
import { buildAkaUrl, parseAkaUrl } from "@/components/video/akaUrls";

const fieldClass = "mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm";

export default function AkaSource() {
  const [src, setSrc] = useState("");
  const [label, setLabel] = useState("");

  const loadPlayer = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const value = String(data.get("link") || "").trim();
    const parsed = parseAkaUrl(value);
    if (parsed) {
      setSrc(parsed.url.href);
      setLabel(parsed.kind === "episode" ? `Season ${parsed.season} Â· Episode ${parsed.episode}` : "Title page");
      return;
    }
    const season = Math.max(1, Number(data.get("season") || 1));
    const episode = Math.max(1, Number(data.get("episode") || 1));
    const url = buildAkaUrl({ slug: value, season, episode });
    if (url) {
      setSrc(url);
      setLabel(`Season ${season} Â· Episode ${episode}`);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-label="Aka video source" className="mb-8 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-4 sm:p-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl">Aka</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Paste an animesalt.cx link â€” an episode link (â€¦/episode/slug-1x1/) or a title link (â€¦/ka) â€” or just the slug with a season and episode below.</p>
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={loadPlayer} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <label className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Link or slug
          <input name="link" required placeholder="https://animesalt.cx/episode/â€¦ or slug" className={fieldClass} />
        </label>
        <label className="w-20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Season<input name="season" type="number" min={1} max={9999} step={1} defaultValue={1} className={fieldClass} /></label>
        <label className="w-20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Episode<input name="episode" type="number" min={1} max={9999} step={1} defaultValue={1} className={fieldClass} /></label>
        <button type="submit" className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"><Play className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /> Load player</button>
      </form>
      {src && <EmbedIframe key={src} src={src} title={`Aka player Â· ${label}`} />}
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">External source: animesalt.cx. Title availability and playback are controlled by the provider.</p>
    </section>
  );
}

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Play } from "lucide-react";

export default function AnimeEpisodeControls({ season, episode, maxEpisode, onSelect }) {
  const submit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    onSelect(Number(data.get("season")), Number(data.get("episode")));
  };
  const fieldClass = "mt-1 w-20 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm text-foreground";
  return (
    <form key={`${season}:${episode}`} onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-4">
      <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">TMDB season<input name="season" type="number" min={0} max={9999} step={1} defaultValue={season} required className={fieldClass} /></label>
      <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Episode<input name="episode" type="number" min={1} max={9999} step={1} defaultValue={episode} required className={fieldClass} /></label>
      <button type="submit" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"><Play className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /> Play</button>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:ml-auto">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onSelect(season, episode - 1)} disabled={episode <= 1} aria-label="Previous episode" className="rounded-full border border-border p-2.5 text-primary disabled:opacity-30"><ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onSelect(season, episode + 1)} disabled={episode >= maxEpisode} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-primary disabled:opacity-30">Next episode <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /></button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

import { Loader2, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import VideoPlayer from "@/components/video/VideoPlayer";
import AnimeEpisodeControls from "@/components/video/AnimeEpisodeControls";
import useAnimePlayback from "@/components/video/useAnimePlayback";

export default function AnimePlayback({ anime, match }) {
  const playback = useAnimePlayback(anime, match);
  if (!playback.ready) return <div role="status" className="grid aspect-video place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-card"><Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-primary" aria-label="Restoring playback" /></div>;
  const maxEpisode = playback.season === match.season && anime.episodes ? Math.max(1, anime.episodes + match.episodeOffset) : 9999;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <VideoPlayer src={playback.src} poster={anime.cover} title={anime.display} startAt={playback.startAt} onProgress={playback.onProgress} />
      {match.mediaType === "tv" && <AnimeEpisodeControls season={playback.season} episode={playback.episode} maxEpisode={maxEpisode} onSelect={playback.selectEpisode} />}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span>{match.mediaType === "tv" ? `Season ${playback.season} Â· Episode ${playback.episode}` : "Movie"}</span>
        <a href={match.tmdbUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-primary hover:underline">TMDB {match.tmdbId} <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /></a>
      </div>
      {playback.historyError && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Watch progress could not be restored or saved. Playback is still available.</p>}
      <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">If playback stalls, switch to AnimeSalt Â· Backup above or reload the selected player. Each provider controls its own availability, quality and subtitles.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {src ? <EmbedIframe key={`${src}:${attempt}`} src={src} title={`AnimeSalt backup${title ? ` Â· ${title}` : ""}`} /> : <div role="status" className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Load the matching AnimeSalt {mediaType === "tv" ? "episode or series" : "movie"} link below to use the backup server. The link is remembered for this title in this browser.</div>}
      <AnimeSaltLinkForm onSelect={onSelect} mediaType={mediaType} defaultValue={pageUrl} />
      {src && <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setAttempt((value) => value + 1)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-primary"><RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Reload backup</button>
        <a href={src} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-primary">Open on AnimeSalt <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /></a>
      </div>}
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{mediaType === "tv" ? `Season ${season} Â· Episode ${episode}. ` : ""}AnimeSalt controls playback and whether its pages can be embedded. Playback position is not synced between servers; Vidstuck remains available above.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/video/AnimeSaltLinkForm.jsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";

export default function AnimeSaltLinkForm({ onSelect, mediaType, defaultValue = "" }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const submit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const value = String(new FormData(event.currentTarget).get("animeSaltLink") || "").trim();
    let url;
    try { url = new URL(value); } catch { setError("Enter a valid AnimeSalt page link."); return; }
    if (url.origin !== "https://animesalt.cx" || url.username || url.password || !/^\/(?:(?:series|movies)\/[a-z0-9-]+|episode\/[a-z0-9-]+-\d+x\d+)\/?$/i.test(url.pathname)) {
      setError("Use an animesalt.cx series, episode or movie link."); return;
    }
    if ((mediaType === "movie" && !url.pathname.startsWith("/movies/")) || (mediaType === "tv" && url.pathname.startsWith("/movies/"))) {
      setError(mediaType === "tv" ? "Use a series or episode link for this title." : "Use a movie link for this title."); return;
    }
    url.search = ""; url.hash = "";
    if (!url.pathname.endsWith("/")) url.pathname += "/";
    setError("");
    onSelect(url.href);
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-2">
      <label htmlFor="anime-salt-link" className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">Matching AnimeSalt episode, series or movie link</label>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
        <input id="anime-salt-link" name="animeSaltLink" type="url" required maxLength={500} defaultValue={defaultValue} placeholder="https://animesalt.cx/episode/â€¦" className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
        <button type="submit" className="rounded-full bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground">Load backup</button>
      </div>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    </form>
  );
}

export default function AnimeScreening({ animeId }) {

  const source = useAnimeSource(animeId);
  if (anime.loading || source.isPending) return <div role="status" className="grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center"><span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-primary" /> Matching anime playerâ€¦</span></div>;
  const error = anime.error || source.error?.message;
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] space-y-6 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">

      </nav>

        <header><p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.26em] text-primary">Anime theatre</p><h1 className="mt-2 font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{anime.item?.display}</h1></header>

          <VidstuckSource />
          <AkaSource />
        </>}
      </>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/video/EmbedIframe.jsx

import { useState } from "react";

/**
 * Reusable, responsive embed player: any https embed or page URL renders in a
 * 16:9 iframe that fills its container. No sandbox attribute â€” restrictive
 * sandboxes break third-party players. Use `key={src}` on the consumer to
 * reload cleanly when the source changes.
 */
export default function EmbedIframe({ src, title = "Embedded video player" }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-black aspect-video">
      {loading && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-card" aria-hidden="true">
          <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-primary/30 border-t-primary" />
        </div>
      )}
      <iframe
        src={src}
        title={title}
        loading="lazy"
        onLoad={() => setLoading(false)}
        allow="autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture"
        allowFullScreen
        referrerPolicy="origin"
        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/video/ServerVideoPlayer.jsx
================================================================================
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import VidstuckPlayer from "@/components/video/VidstuckPlayer";
import AnimeSaltBackup from "@/components/video/AnimeSaltBackup";
import { parseVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";

export default function ServerVideoPlayer({ src, title, startAt = 0, onProgress }) {
  const [server, setServer] = useState("vidstuck");
  const progress = useRef(null);
  const [resume, setResume] = useState(null);
  const source = parseVidstuckUrl(src);
  const storageKey = `pri-anime:animesalt:${source.mediaType}:${source.tmdbId}`;
  const [backupLink, setBackupLink] = useState(() => window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey) || "");
  const selectBackup = (value) => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, value);
    setBackupLink(value);
  };
  const switchServer = (next) => {
    if (next === server) return;
    if (next === "animesalt" && progress.current?.src === src) setResume(progress.current);
    setServer(next);
  };
  const rememberProgress = (position, duration, detail = {}) => {
    if ((detail.season ?? source.season) === source.season && (detail.episode ?? source.episode) === source.episode) progress.current = { src, position };
    onProgress?.(position, duration, detail);
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div role="group" aria-label="Playback server" className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="mr-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Server</span>
        {[{ id: "vidstuck", label: "Vidstuck Â· Main" }, { id: "animesalt", label: "AnimeSalt Â· Backup" }].map((item) => (
          <button key={item.id} type="button" aria-pressed={server === item.id} onClick={() => switchServer(item.id)} className={`rounded-full border px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition ${server === item.id ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border bg-card text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"}`}>{item.label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {server === "vidstuck" ? <VidstuckPlayer src={src} startAt={resume?.src === src ? resume.position : startAt} onProgress={rememberProgress} /> : <AnimeSaltBackup key={src} title={title} pageUrl={backupLink} onSelect={selectBackup} mediaType={source.mediaType} season={source.season} episode={source.episode} />}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/video/VideoPlayer.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Play, Pause, Volume2, VolumeX, Maximize, Gauge, Captions, RotateCcw, AlertTriangle, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import { formatClock } from "@/lib/format";
import ServerVideoPlayer from "@/components/video/ServerVideoPlayer";
import { parseVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";

const RATES = [0.75, 1, 1.25, 1.5, 2];

export default function VideoPlayer({ src, poster, subtitles, title, startAt = 0, onProgress, onEnded }) {
  const videoRef = useRef(null);
  const shellRef = useRef(null);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(0);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
  const [volume, setVolume] = useState(1);
  const [rate, setRate] = useState(1);
  const [captions, setCaptions] = useState(Boolean(subtitles));
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [buffering, setBuffering] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFailed(false);
    setTime(startAt);
    setPlaying(false);
  }, [src, startAt]);

  const toggle = async () => {
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video) return;
    if (video.paused) {
      try {
        await video.play();
      } catch (playError) {
        // A blocked autoplay is not a broken source â€” leave the play button up.
        if (playError?.name !== "NotAllowedError") setFailed(true);
      }
    } else {
      video.pause();
    }
  };

  const seek = (event) => {
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video) return;
    const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    const ratio = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width));
    video.currentTime = ratio * (video.duration || 0);
  };

  const changeRate = () => {
    const next = RATES[(RATES.indexOf(rate) + 1) % RATES.length];
    setRate(next);
    if (videoRef.current) videoRef.current.playbackRate = next;
  };

  const toggleVolume = () => {
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video) return;
    const next = video.muted || video.volume === 0 ? 1 : 0;
    video.muted = next === 0;
    video.volume = next;
    setVolume(next);
  };

  const fullscreen = () => {
    const shell = shellRef.current;
    if (!shell) return;
    if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen();
    else shell.requestFullscreen?.();
  };

  const vidstuck = parseVidstuckUrl(src);
  if (vidstuck) {
    return <ServerVideoPlayer key={`${vidstuck.mediaType}:${vidstuck.tmdbId}`} src={src} title={title} startAt={startAt} onProgress={onProgress} />;
  }

  // An ordinary webpage URL (HTML, not a media file) can never play in a <video>
  // element or a generic iframe â€” say so instead of failing at playback time.
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(src || "") && !/\.(mp4|webm|ogg|ogv|m4v|mov|m3u8|mpd)([?#]|$)/i.test(src)) {
    return (
      <div className="grid aspect-video w-full place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-black px-6 text-center">
        <div>
          <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-6 w-6 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
          <p className="mt-3 font-display text-lg text-white">This source cannot be embedded</p>
          <p className="mt-1 max-w-md text-sm text-white/60">
            It is a webpage link, not a supported video file or official player embed. Add a direct MP4/WebM file or a provider embed URL instead.
          </p>
          <a
            href={src}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer noopener"
            className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/25 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white transition hover:border-primary hover:text-primary"
          >
            Open original page <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div ref={shellRef} className="group relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-black">
      <video
        ref={videoRef}
        src={src}
        poster={poster}
        playsInline
        preload="metadata"
        className="h-full w-full object-contain"
        onLoadedMetadata={(event) => {
          setDuration(event.currentTarget.duration || 0);
          if (startAt > 0 && startAt < event.currentTarget.duration - 5) {
            event.currentTarget.currentTime = startAt;
          }
        }}
        onTimeUpdate={(event) => {
          const current = event.currentTarget.currentTime;
          setTime(current);
          onProgress?.(current, event.currentTarget.duration || 0);
        }}
        onPlay={() => setPlaying(true)}
        onPause={() => setPlaying(false)}
        onWaiting={() => setBuffering(true)}
        onPlaying={() => setBuffering(false)}
        onEnded={() => {
          setPlaying(false);
          onEnded?.();
        }}
        onError={() => setFailed(true)}
      >
        {subtitles ? (
          <track
            kind="captions"
            src={subtitles}
            srcLang="en"
            label="English"
            default={captions}
          />
        ) : null}
      </video>

      {!playing && !failed && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={toggle}
          aria-label="Play video"
          className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-black/25 transition hover:bg-black/15"
        >
          <span className="grid h-16 w-16 place-items-center rounded-full border border-primary/70 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur transition group-hover:scale-105">
            <Play className="ml-1 h-6 w-6 fill-primary text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
        </button>
      )}

      {buffering && !failed && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center">
          <span className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-primary/30 border-t-primary" />
        </div>
      )}

      {failed && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-black/85 px-6 text-center">
          <div>
            <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-6 w-6 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="mt-3 font-display text-lg text-white">This video could not be played.</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-white/60">
              The source may be offline or the provider link expired. Try another title from the theatre.
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setFailed(false);
                videoRef.current?.load();
              }}
              className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/25 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white transition hover:border-primary hover:text-primary"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Retry
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/50 to-transparent px-3 pb-3 pt-10 opacity-100 transition sm:opacity-0 sm:group-hover:opacity-100 sm:group-focus-within:opacity-100">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={seek}
          aria-label="Seek"
          className="group/seek relative block h-4 w-full"
        >
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/25 transition group-hover/seek:h-1.5" />
          <span
            className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 h-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-primary transition group-hover/seek:h-1.5"
            style={{ width: `${duration ? (time / duration) * 100 : 0}%` }}
          />
        </button>

        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-white">
          <button type="button" onClick={toggle} aria-label={playing ? "Pause" : "Play"} className="p-2">
            {playing ? <Pause className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Play className="h-4 w-4" />}
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={toggleVolume} aria-label={volume ? "Mute" : "Unmute"} className="p-2">
            {volume ? <Volume2 className="h-4 w-4" /> : <VolumeX className="h-4 w-4" />}
          </button>
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums text-white/80">
            {formatClock(time)} / {formatClock(duration)}
          </span>

          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
            {subtitles && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  const next = !captions;
                  setCaptions(next);
                  const tracks = videoRef.current?.textTracks;
                  if (tracks) for (const track of tracks) track.mode = next ? "showing" : "hidden";
                }}
                aria-pressed={captions}
                aria-label="Toggle subtitles"
                className={`p-2 ${captions ? "text-primary" : ""}`}
              >
                <Captions className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            )}
            <button type="button" onClick={changeRate} aria-label="Playback speed" className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-2 text-[11px]">
              <Gauge className="h-4 w-4" />
              {rate}Ã—
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={fullscreen} aria-label="Fullscreen" className="p-2">
              <Maximize className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/video/VidstuckPlayer.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ExternalLink, Loader2, RotateCcw } from "lucide-react";
import { parseVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";
import useVidstuckEvents from "@/components/video/useVidstuckEvents";

export default function VidstuckPlayer({ src, startAt = 0, onProgress }) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const [slow, setSlow] = useState(false);
  const frameRef = useRef(null);
  const frameSrc = useMemo(() => {
    const parsed = parseVidstuckUrl(src);
    if (!parsed) return "";
    if (Number.isFinite(startAt) && startAt > 0) parsed.url.searchParams.set("progress", String(Math.floor(startAt)));
    return parsed.url.href;
  }, [src, startAt]);
  useVidstuckEvents(frameRef, frameSrc, onProgress);
  useEffect(() => { setLoaded(false); setFailed(false); setSlow(false); }, [frameSrc, attempt]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (loaded || failed) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { setSlow(true); setLoaded(true); }, 15000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [loaded, failed, frameSrc, attempt]);
  const retry = () => setAttempt((value) => value + 1);
  if (!frameSrc) return <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">This is not a supported Vidstuck embed link.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-background">
        <iframe
          ref={frameRef}
          key={`${frameSrc}:${attempt}`}
          src={frameSrc}
          title="Vidstuck video player"
          className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full border-0"
          allow="autoplay; encrypted-media; fullscreen; picture-in-picture"
          allowFullScreen
          referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
          onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
          onError={() => { setFailed(true); setLoaded(true); }}
        />
        {!loaded && <div role="status" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-background/80"><span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground"><Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-primary" /> Loading Vidstuckâ€¦</span></div>}
        {failed && <div role="alert" className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-background p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Vidstuck could not be loaded. Retry or open the player directly below.</div>}
      </div>
      {slow && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Vidstuck is taking longer than expected. Reload or open its player directly; a loading frame does not confirm stream availability.</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <button type="button" onClick={retry} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-primary"><RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Reload player</button>
        <a href={frameSrc} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-primary">Open on Vidstuck <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /></a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/video/VidstuckSource.jsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Play } from "lucide-react";
import VideoPlayer from "@/components/video/VideoPlayer";
import { buildVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";

const fieldClass = "mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm";

export default function VidstuckSource() {
  const [type, setType] = useState("tv");
  const [src, setSrc] = useState("");
  const loadPlayer = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const url = new URL(buildVidstuckUrl({ mediaType: type, tmdbId: Number(data.get("tmdbId")), season: Number(data.get("season") ?? 1), episode: Number(data.get("episode") ?? 1) }));
    if (type === "tv") url.searchParams.set("episodeSelector", "true");
    setSrc(url.href);
  };

  return (
    <section aria-label="Vidstuck video source" className="mb-8 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-4 sm:p-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl">Vidstuck</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Open a title with its TMDB ID, not its AniList or MyAnimeList ID. <a href="https://www.themoviedb.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-primary hover:underline">Find a TMDB ID</a></p>
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={loadPlayer} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <label className="w-full text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:w-36">Content type
          <select value={type} onChange={(event) => setType(event.target.value)} className={fieldClass}>
            <option value="tv">TV / Anime series</option><option value="movie">Movie</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">TMDB ID
          <input name="tmdbId" required inputMode="numeric" pattern="[1-9][0-9]{0,8}" maxLength={9} placeholder="ID from the TMDB title URL" className={fieldClass} />
        </label>
        {type === "tv" && <>
          <label className="w-20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Season<input name="season" type="number" min={0} max={9999} step={1} required defaultValue={1} className={fieldClass} /></label>
          <label className="w-20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Episode<input name="episode" type="number" min={1} max={9999} step={1} required defaultValue={1} className={fieldClass} /></label>
        </>}
        <button type="submit" className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"><Play className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /> Load player</button>
      </form>
      {src && <VideoPlayer key={src} src={src} />}
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">External source: vidstuck.cc. Title availability and playback are controlled by the provider.</p>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/video/akaUrls.js
================================================================================
const AKA_ORIGIN = "https://animesalt.cx";

export function parseAkaUrl(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.startsWith("https://")) return null;
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(value); } catch { return null; }
  if (url.origin !== AKA_ORIGIN || url.username || url.password) return null;
  const episode = url.pathname.match(/^\/episode\/([a-z0-9-]+?)-(\d+)x(\d+)\/?$/i);
  if (episode) return { url, kind: "episode", slug: episode[1], season: Number(episode[2]), episode: Number(episode[3]) };
  const page = url.pathname.match(/^\/([a-z0-9-]+)\/?$/i);
  if (page) return { url, kind: "page", slug: page[1] };
  return { url, kind: "page" };
}

export function buildAkaUrl({ slug, season = 1, episode = 1 }) {
  const clean = String(slug || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^https?:\/\/animesalt\.cx\//, "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
  if (!clean || clean.includes("/")) return "";
  if (!Number.isInteger(Number(season)) || Number(season) < 1 || !Number.isInteger(Number(episode)) || Number(episode) < 1) return "";
  return `${AKA_ORIGIN}/episode/${clean}-${season}x${episode}/`;
}

export function resolveAkaBackupUrl(value, { mediaType, season, episode }) {
  const parsed = parseAkaUrl(value);
  if (!parsed || parsed.url.search || parsed.url.hash) return "";
  if (mediaType === "movie") return /^\/movies\/[a-z0-9-]+\/$/i.test(parsed.url.pathname) ? parsed.url.href : "";
  if (mediaType !== "tv") return "";
  const series = parsed.url.pathname.match(/^\/series\/([a-z0-9-]+)\/$/i);
  const slug = parsed.kind === "episode" ? parsed.slug : series?.[1];
  return slug ? buildAkaUrl({ slug, season, episode }) : "";
}

FILE: src/components/video/useAnimePlayback.js
================================================================================
import { useRef } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { saveProgress } from "@/lib/library";
import { buildVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";

function coordinate(value, min) {
  if (value == null || value === "") return undefined;
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isInteger(number) && number >= min && number <= 9999 ? number : undefined;
}

export default function useAnimePlayback(anime, match) {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const lastSaved = useRef(0);
  const ownerRef = getProfile().id;
  const history = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["anime-playback-resume", anime.id, ownerRef],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const page = await base44.entities.Progress.filter({ owner_ref: ownerRef, kind: "anime", ref_id: String(anime.id) }, { limit: 1 });
      return page.items?.[0] || null;
    },
    retry: false, staleTime: Infinity, gcTime: 0, refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
  const save = useMutation({ mutationFn: saveProgress });
  const saved = history.data;
  const prior = new URLSearchParams(saved?.link?.split("?")[1] || "");
  const explicit = coordinate(params.get("episode"), 1) !== undefined;
  const season = coordinate(params.get("season"), 0) ?? (!explicit ? coordinate(prior.get("season"), 0) : undefined) ?? match.season;
  const episode = coordinate(params.get("episode"), 1) ?? coordinate(prior.get("episode"), 1) ?? Math.max(1, 1 + match.episodeOffset);
  const sameEpisode = Number(prior.get("season")) === season && Number(prior.get("episode")) === episode;
  const startAt = sameEpisode && saved?.duration - saved?.position > 10 ? saved.position : 0;
  const link = (s, e) => `/watch?anime=${anime.id}&season=${s}&episode=${e}`;
  const selectEpisode = (s, e) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
    next.set("season", String(s)); next.set("episode", String(e));
    lastSaved.current = 0;
    setParams(next);
  };
  const onProgress = (position, duration, detail = {}) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastSaved.current < 5000 || save.isPending) return;
    lastSaved.current = now;
    const s = detail.season ?? season;
    const e = detail.episode ?? episode;
    save.mutate({ kind: "anime", ref_id: anime.id, title: anime.display, cover: anime.cover, episode: s === match.season ? Math.max(1, e - match.episodeOffset) : e, position, duration, link: link(s, e) });
  };
  return {
    season, episode, startAt, selectEpisode, onProgress,
    src: buildVidstuckUrl({ ...match, season, episode }),
    ready: !history.isPending,
    historyError: history.error || save.error,
  };
}

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";

export default function useAnimeSource(animeId) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["anime-stream-source", Number(animeId)],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data } = await base44.functions.invoke("videoProvider", { action: "resolveAnime", animeId: Number(animeId) });
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "The streaming source could not be matched.");
      return data.match || null;
    },
    enabled: Number.isSafeInteger(Number(animeId)) && Number(animeId) > 0,
    staleTime: 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    retry: false,
  });
}

FILE: src/components/video/useVidstuckEvents.js
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { parseVidstuckUrl } from "@/components/video/vidstuckUrls";

export default function useVidstuckEvents(frameRef, src, onProgress) {
  const callback = useRef(onProgress);
  callback.current = onProgress;
  useEffect(() => {
    const expected = parseVidstuckUrl(src);
    if (!expected) return;
    const receive = (event) => {
      if (event.origin !== expected.url.origin || event.source !== frameRef.current?.contentWindow) return;
      let data = event.data;
      if (typeof data === "string") {
        if (data.length > 16000) return;
        try { data = JSON.parse(data); } catch { return; }
      }
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data)) return;
      if (Number(data.id) !== expected.tmdbId || data.type !== expected.mediaType) return;
      const position = Number(data.timestamp);
      const duration = Number(data.duration);
      const season = Number(data.season ?? expected.season);
      const episode = Number(data.episode ?? expected.episode);
      if (!Number.isFinite(position) || !Number.isFinite(duration) || position < 0 || duration <= 0 || position > duration + 5) return;
      if (!Number.isInteger(season) || season < 0 || !Number.isInteger(episode) || episode < 1) return;
      callback.current?.(position, duration, { season, episode });
    };
    window.addEventListener("message", receive);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("message", receive);
  }, [frameRef, src]);
}

FILE: src/components/video/vidstuckUrls.js
================================================================================
export function parseVidstuckUrl(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.startsWith("https://")) return null;
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(value); } catch { return null; }
  if (url.origin !== "https://vidstuck.cc" || url.username || url.password) return null;
  const movie = url.pathname.match(/^\/embed\/movie\/([1-9]\d*)\/?$/);
  const tv = url.pathname.match(/^\/embed\/tv\/([1-9]\d*)\/(\d+)\/([1-9]\d*)\/?$/);
  if (!movie && !tv) return null;
  return { url, mediaType: movie ? "movie" : "tv", tmdbId: Number((movie || tv)[1]), season: tv ? Number(tv[2]) : 1, episode: tv ? Number(tv[3]) : 1 };
}

export function buildVidstuckUrl({ mediaType, tmdbId, season = 1, episode = 1 }) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(Number(tmdbId)) || Number(tmdbId) <= 0) return "";
  if (!["movie", "tv"].includes(mediaType)) return "";
  if (!Number.isInteger(Number(season)) || Number(season) < 0 || !Number.isInteger(Number(episode)) || Number(episode) < 1) return "";
  const path = mediaType === "movie" ? `movie/${tmdbId}` : `tv/${tmdbId}/${season}/${episode}`;
  const url = new URL(`/embed/${path}`, "https://vidstuck.cc");
  url.searchParams.set("branding", "PRI-ANIME");
  url.searchParams.set("subtitle", "english");
  return url.href;
}

FILE: src/hooks/use-mobile.jsx

import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}

FILE: src/hooks/use-size.jsx
================================================================================
import * as React from "react"

export function useSize(ref) {
  const [size, setSize] = React.useState(null)

  // useLayoutEffect (not useEffect): the initial measurement must land before
  // the browser paints, so consumers can render their real content on the
  // very first painted frame instead of a guess. A ResizeObserver's first
  // callback arrives too late for that â€” by then an <img> src guess has
  // already been dispatched to the network.
  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current
    if (!element) return

    const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect()
    setSize({ width: rect.width, height: rect.height })

    const observer = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => {
      const { width, height } = entry.contentRect
      setSize({ width, height })
    })

    observer.observe(element)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [ref])

  return size
}

FILE: src/hooks/useAnime.js

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { callAniList } from "@/lib/animeApi";

export function useAnimeList(body, enabled = true) {
  const key = JSON.stringify(body);
  const [state, setState] = useState({ items: [], loading: enabled, error: null, pageInfo: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    let alive = true;
    setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, loading: true, error: null }));
    callAniList(JSON.parse(key))
      .then((data) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setState({ items: data.items || [], loading: false, error: null, pageInfo: data.pageInfo || null });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setState({ items: [], loading: false, error: error.message, pageInfo: null });
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [key, enabled]);

  return state;
}

export function useAnimeDetail(id) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ item: null, loading: true, error: null });

  const reload = useCallback(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    setState({ item: null, loading: true, error: null });
    callAniList({ action: "by_id", id })
      .then((data) => setState({ item: data.item, loading: false, error: null }))
      .catch((error) => setState({ item: null, loading: false, error: error.message }));
  }, [id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    reload();
  }, [reload]);

  return { ...state, reload };
}

export function usePaginatedAnime(body) {
  const key = JSON.stringify(body);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [retryNonce, setRetryNonce] = useState(0);
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState({ loading: true, error: null, hasNext: false, total: 0 });

  // A new query, genre or sort always restarts from page 1 and clears the
  // previous list before the fresh request goes out.
  useEffect(() => {
    setPage(1);
    setItems([]);
  }, [key]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true; // stale responses of outdated requests can never overwrite newer results
    setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, loading: true, error: null }));
    callAniList({ ...JSON.parse(key), page })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setItems((prev) => (page === 1 ? data.items || [] : [...prev, ...(data.items || [])]));
        setState({
          loading: false,
          error: null,
          hasNext: Boolean(data.pageInfo?.hasNextPage),
          total: data.pageInfo?.total || 0,
        });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        // A failed request is an error state, never a zero-results state.
        const offline = typeof navigator !== "undefined" && !navigator.onLine;
        setState((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          loading: false,
          error: offline
            ? "You appear to be offline. Check your connection, then retry."
            : error.message,
        }));
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [key, page, retryNonce]);

  const loadMore = useCallback(() => setPage((current) => current + 1), []);
  const retry = useCallback(() => setRetryNonce((current) => current + 1), []);

  return { items, ...state, loadMore, retry, reset: () => setPage(1) };
}

FILE: src/hooks/useHoverPreview.js
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

const OPEN_DELAY = 200;
const CLOSE_DELAY = 200;

export function useHoverPreview() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const openTimer = useRef(null);
  const closeTimer = useRef(null);

  const clearOpen = useCallback(() => {
    if (openTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(openTimer.current);
      openTimer.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const clearClose = useCallback(() => {
    if (closeTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(closeTimer.current);
      closeTimer.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const openPreview = useCallback(() => {
    clearClose();
    if (openTimer.current) return;
    openTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      openTimer.current = null;
      setOpen(true);
    }, OPEN_DELAY);
  }, [clearClose]);

  const scheduleClose = useCallback(() => {
    clearOpen();
    if (closeTimer.current) return;
    closeTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      closeTimer.current = null;
      setOpen(false);
    }, CLOSE_DELAY);
  }, [clearOpen]);

  const cancelClose = useCallback(clearClose, [clearClose]);

  const closeNow = useCallback(() => {
    clearOpen();
    clearClose();
    setOpen(false);
  }, [clearOpen, clearClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return undefined;
    const onKeyDown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") closeNow();
    };
    const onScroll = () => closeNow();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, true);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll, true);
    };
  }, [open, closeNow]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      clearOpen();
      clearClose();
    },
    [clearOpen, clearClose]
  );

  return { open, openPreview, scheduleClose, cancelClose, closeNow };
}

FILE: src/hooks/useNotifications.js
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";

const SEEN_KEY = "pri-anime:seen-notifications";

/**
 * Notifications are derived from real app state: finished AI render jobs and
 * the newest published reels. Nothing is invented â€” an empty account shows an
 * empty tray.
 */
export function useNotifications() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [seenAt, setSeenAt] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem(SEEN_KEY) || "");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [jobs, reels] = await Promise.all([
        base44.entities.VideoJob.list("-created_date", 5),
        base44.entities.Reel.filter({ status: "published" }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 4 }),
      ]);

      const jobItems = (Array.isArray(jobs) ? jobs : jobs?.items || [])
        .filter((job) => job.status === "completed" || job.status === "failed")
        .map((job) => ({
          id: `job-${job.id}`,
          title: job.status === "completed" ? "Your AI film is ready" : "A render job failed",
          body:
            job.status === "completed"
              ? `${job.style || "Cinematic"} Â· ${job.aspect_ratio || "16:9"}`
              : job.error || "The provider rejected the request.",
          to: "/creator",
          at: job.updated_date || job.created_date,
        }));

      const reelItems = (reels?.items || []).map((reel) => ({
        id: `reel-${reel.id}`,
        title: "New reel in the vortex",
        body: `${reel.title} â€” ${reel.creator_name || "creators"}`,
        to: "/reels",
        at: reel.created_date,
      }));

      setItems(
        [...jobItems, ...reelItems]
          .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.at || 0) - new Date(a.at || 0))
          .slice(0, 8)
      );
    } catch {
      setItems([]);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    const id = setInterval(load, 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [load]);

  const unread = items.filter((item) => !seenAt || new Date(item.at || 0) > new Date(seenAt)).length;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (items.length && !seenAt) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      localStorage.setItem(SEEN_KEY, now);
    }
  }, [items, seenAt]);

  return { items, unread };
}

export default useNotifications;

FILE: src/hooks/useWatchlist.js
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { listSaved, toggleSaved } from "@/lib/library";

export function useWatchlist(kind) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const rows = await listSaved(kind);
      setItems(rows);
    } catch {
      setItems([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [kind]);

  useEffect(() => {
    refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  const isSaved = useCallback(
    (animeId) => items.some((row) => row.anime_id === animeId),
    [items]
  );

  const toggle = useCallback(
    async (anime) => {
      const active = await toggleSaved(anime, kind);
      await refresh();
      return active;
    },
    [kind, refresh]
  );

  return { items, loading, isSaved, toggle, refresh };
}

FILE: src/lib/AuthContext.jsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { appParams } from '@/lib/app-params';

const AuthContext = createContext();

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [isLoadingAuth, setIsLoadingAuth] = useState(true);
  const [isLoadingPublicSettings, setIsLoadingPublicSettings] = useState(true);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState(null);
  const [authChecked, setAuthChecked] = useState(false);
  const [appPublicSettings, setAppPublicSettings] = useState(null); // Contains only { id, public_settings }

  useEffect(() => {
    checkAppState();
  }, []);

  const checkAppState = async () => {
    try {
      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(true);
      setAuthError(null);
      
      try {
        const publicSettings = await base44.app.getPublicSettings();
        setAppPublicSettings(publicSettings);
        
        // If we got the app public settings successfully, check if user is authenticated
        if (appParams.token) {
          await checkUserAuth();
        } else {
          setIsLoadingAuth(false);
          setIsAuthenticated(false);
          setAuthChecked(true);
        }
        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
      } catch (appError) {
        console.error('App state check failed:', appError);
        
        // Handle app-level errors
        if (appError.status === 403 && appError.data?.extra_data?.reason) {
          const reason = appError.data.extra_data.reason;
          if (reason === 'auth_required') {
            setAuthError({
              type: 'auth_required',
              message: 'Authentication required'
            });
          } else if (reason === 'user_not_registered') {
            setAuthError({
              type: 'user_not_registered',
              message: 'User not registered for this app'
            });
          } else {
            setAuthError({
              type: reason,
              message: appError.message
            });
          }
        } else {
          setAuthError({
            type: 'unknown',
            message: appError.message || 'Failed to load app'
          });
        }
        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
        setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Unexpected error:', error);
      setAuthError({
        type: 'unknown',
        message: error.message || 'An unexpected error occurred'
      });
      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
    }
  };

  const checkUserAuth = async () => {
    try {
      // Now check if the user is authenticated
      setIsLoadingAuth(true);
      const currentUser = await base44.auth.me();
      setUser(currentUser);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      setAuthChecked(true);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('User auth check failed:', error);
      setIsLoadingAuth(false);
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
      setAuthChecked(true);
      
      // If user auth fails, it might be an expired token
      if (error.status === 401 || error.status === 403) {
        setAuthError({
          type: 'auth_required',
          message: 'Authentication required'
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const logout = (shouldRedirect = true) => {
    setUser(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
    
    if (shouldRedirect) {
      // Use the SDK's logout method which handles token cleanup and redirect
      base44.auth.logout(window.location.href);
    } else {
      // Just remove the token without redirect
      base44.auth.logout();
    }
  };

  const navigateToLogin = () => {
    // Use the SDK's redirectToLogin method
    base44.auth.redirectToLogin(window.location.href);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ 
      user, 
      isAuthenticated, 
      isLoadingAuth,
      isLoadingPublicSettings,
      authError,
      appPublicSettings,
      authChecked,
      logout,
      navigateToLogin,
      checkUserAuth,
      checkAppState
    }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';


export default function PageNotFound({}) {
    const location = useLocation();
    const pageName = location.pathname.substring(1);

    const { data: authData, isFetched } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['user'],
        queryFn: async () => {
            try {
                const user = await base44.auth.me();
                return { user, isAuthenticated: true };
            } catch (error) {
                return { user: null, isAuthenticated: false };
            }
        }
    });
    
    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-slate-50">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full">
                <div className="text-center space-y-6">
                    {/* 404 Error Code */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <h1 className="text-7xl font-light text-slate-300">404</h1>
                        <div className="h-0.5 w-16 bg-slate-200 mx-auto"></div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Main Message */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <h2 className="text-2xl font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Page Not Found
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                            The page <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">"{pageName}"</span> could not be found in this application.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Admin Note */}
                    {isFetched && authData.isAuthenticated && authData.user?.role === 'admin' && (
                        <div className="mt-8 p-4 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                            <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5">
                                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orange-400"></div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-left space-y-1">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Admin Note</p>
                                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                                        This could mean that the AI hasn't implemented this page yet. Ask it to implement it in the chat.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    
                    {/* Action Button */}
                    <div className="pt-6">
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => window.location.href = '/'} 
                            className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-slate-500"
                        >
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M3 12l2-2m0 0l7-7 7 7M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h3m10-11l2 2m-2-2v10a1 1 0 01-1 1h-3m-6 0a1 1 0 001-1v-4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v4a1 1 0 001 1m-6 0h6" />
                            </svg>
                            Go Home
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

FILE: src/lib/animeApi.js
