import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { searchVariants, withRetries } from "@/lib/searchUtils";

const DIRECT_URL = "https://graphql.anilist.co";
const TTL = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const SERVER_KEY = "pri:anilist:server-mode";
const cache = new Map();

const CORE = `id title { romaji english native } coverImage { extraLarge large color } bannerImage description(asHtml: false) genres averageScore meanScore popularity episodes duration status format season seasonYear startDate { year month day } studios(isMain: true) { nodes { name } } trailer { id site } nextAiringEpisode { episode timeUntilAiring }`;

const LIST_QUERY = `query ($page: Int, $perPage: Int, $search: String, $genre: String, $season: MediaSeason, $seasonYear: Int, $sort: [MediaSort]) {
  Page(page: $page, perPage: $perPage) {
    pageInfo { total currentPage lastPage hasNextPage }
    media(type: ANIME, isAdult: false, search: $search, genre: $genre, season: $season, seasonYear: $seasonYear, sort: $sort) { ${CORE} }
  }
}`;

const DETAIL_QUERY = `query ($id: Int) {
  Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
    ${CORE}
    duration
    source
    characters(sort: [ROLE, RELEVANCE], perPage: 8) { nodes { id name { full } image { large } } }
    relations { edges { relationType node { id type title { romaji english } coverImage { large } } } }
    recommendations(perPage: 8, sort: RATING_DESC) { nodes { mediaRecommendation { id title { romaji english } coverImage { large extraLarge } averageScore } } }
  }
}`;

export function currentSeason(date = new Date()) {
  const month = date.getUTCMonth();
  const season = month < 3 ? "WINTER" : month < 6 ? "SPRING" : month < 9 ? "SUMMER" : "FALL";
  return { season, year: date.getUTCFullYear() };
}

export function normalize(media) {
  if (!media) return null;
  return {
    id: media.id,
    source: "anilist",
    display: media.title?.english || media.title?.romaji || "Untitled",
    titleRomaji: media.title?.romaji || "",
    cover: media.coverImage?.extraLarge || media.coverImage?.large || "",
    accent: media.coverImage?.color || "",
    banner: media.bannerImage || media.coverImage?.extraLarge || "",
    description: (media.description || "").replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, " ").replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ""),
    genres: media.genres || [],
    score: media.averageScore ?? null,
    popularity: media.popularity ?? 0,
    episodes: media.episodes ?? null,
    duration: media.duration ?? null,
    status: media.status || "",
    format: media.format || "",
    season: media.season || "",
    seasonYear: media.seasonYear ?? null,
    studios: (media.studios?.nodes || []).map((node) => node.name),
    trailer: media.trailer || null,
    nextEpisode: media.nextAiringEpisode || null,
    characters: (media.characters?.nodes || []).map((node) => ({
      id: node.id,
      name: node.name?.full || "",
      image: node.image?.large || "",
    })),
    relations: (media.relations?.edges || [])
      .filter((edge) => edge.node?.type === "ANIME")
      .slice(0, 8)
      .map((edge) => normalize({ ...edge.node, description: "" })),
    recommendations: (media.recommendations?.nodes || [])
      .map((node) => node.mediaRecommendation)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((item) => normalize(item)),
  };
}

let serverMode = typeof sessionStorage !== "undefined" && sessionStorage.getItem(SERVER_KEY) !== "off";

async function tryServer(body) {
  if (!serverMode) return null;
  try {
    const response = await base44.functions.invoke("anilist", body);
    if (response?.data?.ok) return response.data;
    serverMode = false;
    sessionStorage.setItem(SERVER_KEY, "off");
    return null;
  } catch {
    serverMode = false;
    sessionStorage.setItem(SERVER_KEY, "off");
    return null;
  }
}

async function fetchGraphQL(query, variables) {
  const response = await fetch(DIRECT_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables }),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000),
  });
  // HTTP errors and GraphQL errors are classified separately, and every
  // failure carries whether it is safe to retry.
  if (response.status === 429) {
    const error = new Error("AniList is temporarily limiting requests. Try again shortly.");
    error.status = 429;
    error.retryable = true;
    throw error;
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(`AniList responded ${response.status}.`);
    error.status = response.status;
    error.retryable = response.status >= 500;
    throw error;
  }
  let payload;
  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    // An HTTP 200 with an unreadable body (e.g. a proxy/CDN interstitial) is
    // NOT a genuine zero-results answer â€” it is a transient failure.
    const error = new Error("AniList returned an unreadable response.");
    error.retryable = true;
    throw error;
  }
  if (payload.errors?.length) {
    const error = new Error(payload.errors[0].message || "AniList rejected the query.");
    error.status = 400;
    error.retryable = false;
    throw error;
  }
  if (!payload.data) {
    const error = new Error("AniList returned no data for this query.");
    error.retryable = true;
    throw error;
  }
  return payload.data;
}

// An HTTP success does not mean the query succeeded: validate the payload
// shape, retry transient failures with bounded backoff, and time out hung
// requests so they can never masquerade as an empty catalogue.
async function graphql(query, variables) {
  return withRetries(() => fetchGraphQL(query, variables));
}

function buildListVariables(body) {
  const action = body.action || "trending";
  const variables = {
    page: Math.min(Math.max(Number(body.page) || 1, 1), 50),
    perPage: Math.min(Math.max(Number(body.perPage) || 20, 1), 25),
    search: null,
    genre: null,
    season: null,
    seasonYear: null,
    sort: ["POPULARITY_DESC"],
  };

  if (action === "trending") variables.sort = ["TRENDING_DESC"];
  else if (action === "top") variables.sort = ["SCORE_DESC"];
  else if (action === "seasonal") {
    const now = currentSeason();
    variables.season = (body.season || now.season).toUpperCase();
    variables.seasonYear = Number(body.year) || now.year;
    variables.sort = ["POPULARITY_DESC"];
  } else if (action === "genre") {
    variables.genre = body.genre || "Action";
    variables.sort = ["POPULARITY_DESC"];
  } else if (action === "search") {
    variables.search = (body.q || "").trim() || null;
    if (!variables.search) return null;
    variables.sort = ["SEARCH_MATCH"];
    if (body.genre) variables.genre = body.genre;
  }

  return variables;
}

export async function callAniList(body) {
  const key = JSON.stringify(body);
  const hit = cache.get(key);
  if (hit && hit.expires > Date.now()) return hit.value;

  const serverResult = await tryServer(body);
  let result;
  // A server search that comes back empty is NOT trusted as final â€” AniList
  // intermittently serves empty pages to valid queries â€” so it falls through
  // to the validated direct search with variants below.
  if (serverResult && (serverResult.items?.length || body.action !== "search")) {
    result = {
      items: (serverResult.items || []).map(normalize),
      item: serverResult.item ? normalize(serverResult.item) : null,
      pageInfo: serverResult.pageInfo || null,
    };
    if (serverResult.item) {
      result.item = {
        ...normalize(serverResult.item),
        characters: serverResult.item.characters || [],
        relations: serverResult.item.relations || [],
        recommendations: (serverResult.item.recommendations || []).map(normalize),
      };
    }
  } else if (body.action === "by_id") {
    const id = Number(body.id);
    if (!id) throw new Error("A numeric anime id is required.");
    const data = await graphql(DETAIL_QUERY, { id });
    if (!data?.Media) throw new Error("That anime could not be found.");
    result = { item: normalize(data.Media), items: [], pageInfo: null };
  } else if (body.action === "search") {
    const variables = buildListVariables(body);
    if (!variables) return { items: [], pageInfo: null, item: null };
    // AniList's search intermittently answers a valid query with an empty
    // page. Each attempt is validated (payload.data.Page), then normalized
    // variants of the same term are tried before accepting a genuine
    // zero-result search. Same provider, same filters â€” no ID mismatch.
    result = { items: [], pageInfo: null, item: null };
    const variants = searchVariants(variables.search);
    // The original term gets one extra attempt (AniList can empty a single
    // valid query), then normalized variants are explored â€” at most 3 calls.
    const attempts = variants.length > 1 ? [variants[0], ...variants] : [variants[0], variants[0]];
    for (const variant of attempts) {
      const data = await graphql(LIST_QUERY, { ...variables, search: variant });
      const items = (data?.Page?.media || []).map(normalize).filter(Boolean);
      if (items.length > 0) {
        result = { items, pageInfo: data?.Page?.pageInfo || null, item: null };
        break;
      }
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 250));
    }
  } else {
    const variables = buildListVariables(body);
    if (!variables) return { items: [], pageInfo: null, item: null };
    const data = await graphql(LIST_QUERY, variables);
    if (!data?.Page) throw new Error("AniList returned an invalid catalogue response.");
    result = {
      items: (data.Page.media || []).map(normalize).filter(Boolean),
      pageInfo: data.Page.pageInfo || null,
      item: null,
    };
  }

  if (cache.size > 200) cache.clear();
  // A genuinely empty list is cached only briefly, so a transient provider
  // hiccup can never stick as "zero results" for the full TTL. Failures are
  // never cached at all (the throw above bypasses this line).
  const ttl = result.items?.length ? TTL : 30 * 1000;
  cache.set(key, { value: result, expires: Date.now() + ttl });
  return result;
}

const malCache = new Map();

// MyAnimeList ratings come from Jikan (public MyAnimeList data, no key in the
// browser) with the keyed backend as a fallback.
async function jikanRating(title) {
  const response = await fetch(
    `https://api.jikan.moe/v4/anime?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}&limit=1&sfw=true`,
    { headers: { Accept: "application/json" }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000) },
  );
  if (!response.ok) return null;
  const payload = await response.json();
  const hit = payload?.data?.[0];
  if (!hit?.score) return null;
  return { score: hit.score, rank: hit.rank ?? null, id: hit.mal_id ?? null };
}

export async function fetchMalRating(title) {
  if (!title) return null;
  if (malCache.has(title)) return malCache.get(title);

  let rating = null;
  try {
    rating = await jikanRating(title);
  } catch {
    rating = null;
  }

  if (!rating) {
    try {
      const response = await base44.functions.invoke("mal", { action: "search", q: title, limit: 3 });
      const data = response?.data;
      rating = data?.ok && data.items?.[0]
        ? { score: data.items[0].malScore, rank: data.items[0].rank, id: data.items[0].id }
        : null;
    } catch {
      rating = null;
    }
  }

  malCache.set(title, rating);
  return rating;
}

export async function fetchProviderStatus() {
  try {
    const response = await base44.functions.invoke("videoProvider", { action: "status" });
    return response?.data || null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

import { getAccessToken } from '@base44/sdk';

const isNode = typeof window === 'undefined';

const isClearAccessTokenRequested = () =>
	!isNode && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("clear_access_token") === 'true';

const clearStoredAccessToken = () => {
	window.localStorage.removeItem('base44_access_token');
	window.localStorage.removeItem('token');
}

const getAppParams = () => {
	if (isClearAccessTokenRequested()) {
		clearStoredAccessToken();
	}
	return {
		appId: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_ID,
		token: getAccessToken(),
		functionsVersion: (!isNode && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("functions_version")?.trim()) || import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_FUNCTIONS_VERSION,
		appBaseUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL,
	}
}


export const appParams = {
	...getAppParams()
}

FILE: src/lib/authReturnTo.js
================================================================================
// Shared by the auth pages (Login, Register, and any page that resumes a flow
// after sign-in, e.g. the MCP OAuth consent page). Keep the redirect
// validation in one place â€” it is security-sensitive and easy to drift.

// Resolve ?returnTo= to a safe same-origin path, else "/".
//
// The same-origin check alone is not enough: a value like /.//evil.com or
// /\evil.com parses same-origin but normalizes to a protocol-relative
// //evil.com when assigned to location.href â€” an open redirect. So require the
// resolved path to be exactly one leading slash (no "//" prefix, no backslash).
export function safeReturnTo() {
  const raw = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("returnTo");
  if (!raw) return "/";
  try {
    const url = new URL(raw, window.location.origin);
    if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return "/";
    // Only access_token/clear_access_token are still URL-read by app-params.js, but the
    // whole bootstrap set stays stripped: one going back to a URL read must not silently
    // become injectable again. Normal app-flow params (e.g. the OAuth consent ctx) are kept.
    for (const p of ["access_token", "clear_access_token", "app_id", "app_base_url", "functions_version", "from_url"]) {
      url.searchParams.delete(p);
    }
    const path = url.pathname + url.search;
    if (!path.startsWith("/") || path.startsWith("//") || path.includes("\\")) return "/";
    return path;
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}

FILE: src/lib/format.js

export function formatClock(seconds) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) return "0:00";
  const total = Math.floor(seconds);
  const hours = Math.floor(total / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = total % 60;
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(secs).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  return `${minutes}:${String(secs).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export function formatCount(value) {
  const number = Number(value) || 0;
  if (number >= 1000000) return `${(number / 1000000).toFixed(1)}M`;
  if (number >= 1000) return `${(number / 1000).toFixed(1)}K`;
  return String(number);
}

export function percent(position, duration) {
  if (!duration) return 0;
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (position / duration) * 100));
}

export function relativeDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(value).getTime();
  const minutes = Math.round(diff / 60000);
  if (minutes < 1) return "just now";
  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
  const hours = Math.round(minutes / 60);
  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
  const days = Math.round(hours / 24);
  if (days < 30) return `${days}d ago`;
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString();
}

export function seasonLabel(season, year) {
  if (!season) return year ? String(year) : "";
  const title = { WINTER: "Winter", SPRING: "Spring", SUMMER: "Summer", FALL: "Fall" }[season] || season;
  return `${title} ${year || ""}`.trim();
}

import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";

const owner = () => getProfile().id;

export async function listSaved(kind) {
  const page = await base44.entities.Watchlist.filter(
    { owner_ref: owner(), kind },
    { sort: "-created_date", limit: 200 }
  );
  return page.items || [];
}

export async function toggleSaved(anime, kind) {
  const existing = await base44.entities.Watchlist.filter(
    { owner_ref: owner(), anime_id: anime.id, kind },
    { limit: 1 }
  );
  const found = existing.items?.[0];
  if (found) {
    await base44.entities.Watchlist.delete(found.id);
    return false;
  }
  await base44.entities.Watchlist.create({
    owner_ref: owner(),
    kind,
    anime_id: anime.id,
    title: anime.display || anime.title,
    cover: anime.cover || "",
    banner: anime.banner || "",
    score: anime.score ?? undefined,
    episodes: anime.episodes ?? undefined,
    format: anime.format || "",
    genres: (anime.genres || []).slice(0, 6),
  });
  return true;
}

export async function listProgress(kind) {
  const page = await base44.entities.Progress.filter(
    { owner_ref: owner(), kind },
    { sort: "-updated_date", limit: 100 }
  );
  return page.items || [];
}

export async function saveProgress(entry) {
  const existing = await base44.entities.Progress.filter(
    { owner_ref: owner(), kind: entry.kind, ref_id: String(entry.ref_id) },
    { limit: 1 }
  );
  const payload = {
    title: entry.title || "",
    cover: entry.cover || "",
    episode: entry.episode ?? 0,
    position: Math.round(entry.position ?? 0),
    duration: Math.round(entry.duration ?? 0),
    link: entry.link || "",
  };
  const found = existing.items?.[0];
  if (found) return base44.entities.Progress.update(found.id, payload);
  return base44.entities.Progress.create({
    owner_ref: owner(),
    kind: entry.kind,
    ref_id: String(entry.ref_id),
    ...payload,
  });
}

export async function clearProgress(entryId) {
  return base44.entities.Progress.delete(entryId);
}

export async function recordAnimeView(anime) {
  try {
    const watched = await base44.entities.Progress.count({ owner_ref: owner(), kind: "anime", ref_id: String(anime.id), episode: { $gt: 0 } });
    if (watched > 0) return;
    await saveProgress({
      kind: "anime",
      ref_id: anime.id,
      title: anime.display,
      cover: anime.cover,
      episode: 0,
      position: 0,
      duration: 0,
      link: `/anime/${anime.id}`,
    });
  } catch {
    /* view tracking is best-effort */
  }
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const PREFIX = "pri-anime:pref:";

export const DEFAULT_PREFS = {
  language: "en",
  reelMuted: true,
  reduceMotion: false,
  autoplayReels: true,
  notifications: true,
  dataSaver: false,
  subtitles: true,
};

export function usePref(key) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFIX + key);
      return raw === null ? DEFAULT_PREFS[key] : JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return DEFAULT_PREFS[key];
    }
  });

  const update = useCallback(
    (next) => {
      setValue(next);
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(PREFIX + key, JSON.stringify(next));
      } catch {
        /* storage full or blocked */
      }
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("pri-pref-change", { detail: { key, value: next } }));
    },
    [key]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (event) => {
      if (event.detail?.key === key) setValue(event.detail.value);
    };
    window.addEventListener("pri-pref-change", sync);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("pri-pref-change", sync);
  }, [key]);

  return [value, update];
}

  { id: "es", label: "EspaÃ±ol" },
];

FILE: src/lib/profile.js

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "pri-anime:profile";
const EVENT = "pri-profile-change";

const ADJECTIVES = ["Silent", "Gilded", "Crimson", "Astral", "Hollow", "Radiant", "Umbra", "Vesper"];
const NOUNS = ["Ronin", "Oracle", "Wanderer", "Scribe", "Sentinel", "Dreamer", "Kitsune", "Archivist"];

function randomName() {
  const a = ADJECTIVES[Math.floor(Math.random() * ADJECTIVES.length)];
  const n = NOUNS[Math.floor(Math.random() * NOUNS.length)];
  return `${a} ${n}`;
}

export function createProfile() {
  return {
    id: `viewer_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}${Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4)}`,
    name: randomName(),
    bio: "Wandering the gilded theater.",
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

export function getProfile() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (parsed?.id) return parsed;
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore corrupt storage */
  }
  const fresh = createProfile();
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(fresh));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  return fresh;
}

export function saveProfile(profile) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(profile));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(EVENT, { detail: profile }));
}

export function useProfile() {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(getProfile);

  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = (event) => setProfile(event.detail);
    window.addEventListener(EVENT, sync);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(EVENT, sync);
  }, []);

  const update = useCallback((patch) => {
    const next = { ...getProfile(), ...patch };
    saveProfile(next);
    return next;
  }, []);

  return [profile, update];
}

/**
 * Pure helpers for the anime search pipeline: query variants, retryable
 * error classification and bounded exponential-backoff retries.
 * No platform imports â€” safe to unit-test in isolation.
 */

/**
 * Builds normalized variants of a search term (used in order, first match wins).
 * Trims whitespace, collapses runs of spaces, strips punctuation and drops
 * leading articles â€” the original term is always the first variant.
 */
export function searchVariants(rawQuery) {
  const trimmed = String(rawQuery || "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return [];
  const collapsed = trimmed.replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  const variants = [collapsed];
  const noPunctuation = collapsed
    .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
  if (noPunctuation && !variants.includes(noPunctuation)) variants.push(noPunctuation);
  const withoutArticles = collapsed
    .replace(/\b(the|a|an)\b\s*/gi, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
  if (withoutArticles && !variants.includes(withoutArticles)) variants.push(withoutArticles);
  return variants;
}

/**
 * Decides whether a failed API call is worth retrying. Explicit markers win;
 * network failures, timeouts, rate limits and server errors retry, while
 * invalid queries and provider rejections (4xx) do not.
 */
export function isRetryableFetchError(error) {
  if (!error) return false;
  if (error.retryable === false) return false;
  if (error.retryable === true) return true;
  if (error.name === "TypeError") return true; // fetch network failure
  if (error.name === "TimeoutError" || error.name === "AbortError") return true;
  if (Number.isFinite(error.status)) return error.status === 429 || error.status >= 500;
  return false;
}

const defaultSleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

/**
 * Runs `run(attempt)` with bounded retries. Only retryable failures retry,
 * with exponential backoff (500ms, 1000ms, â€¦) and a longer, respectful pause
 * after rate limits. Non-retryable errors fail fast; retries are never
 * infinite.
 */
export async function withRetries(run, { retries = 2, sleep = defaultSleep } = {}) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      return await run(attempt);
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      if (attempt === retries || !isRetryableFetchError(error)) break;
      await sleep(error.status === 429 ? 1200 : 500 * 2 ** attempt);
    }
  }
  throw lastError;
}

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";

export const THEMES = [
  { id: "royal", name: "Royal Gold", note: "Gilded midnight", mode: "dark", dots: ["#D4AF37", "#3D1D5A", "#07050A"] },
  { id: "void", name: "Void Cyan", note: "Starlit abyss", mode: "dark", dots: ["#38D9E8", "#3B2A6B", "#050418"] },
  { id: "crimson", name: "Crimson Dusk", note: "Ember court", mode: "dark", dots: ["#E0455F", "#5A1B2A", "#120406"] },
  { id: "jade", name: "Jade Empire", note: "Spirit forest", mode: "dark", dots: ["#38C98F", "#12352A", "#04120C"] },
  { id: "parchment", name: "Parchment", note: "Ivory archive", mode: "light", dots: ["#8A6A22", "#E7DCC8", "#F7F3EB"] },
  { id: "frost", name: "Frost Ivory", note: "Daylit theatre", mode: "light", dots: ["#1D6FA8", "#DCE7F1", "#F4F8FC"] },
];

const STORAGE_KEY = "pri-anime:theme";
const ThemeContext = createContext(null);

function applyTheme(id) {
  const theme = THEMES.find((t) => t.id === id) || THEMES[0];
  const root = document.documentElement;
  root.setAttribute("data-theme", theme.id);
  root.classList.toggle("dark", theme.mode === "dark");
  root.style.colorScheme = theme.mode;
}

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setThemeState] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return "royal";
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || "royal";
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    applyTheme(theme);
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const setTheme = useCallback((id) => {
    if (THEMES.some((t) => t.id === id)) setThemeState(id);
  }, []);

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, setTheme, themes: THEMES }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  return useContext(ThemeContext) || { theme: "royal", setTheme: () => {}, themes: THEMES };
}

export const currentThemeMeta = (id) => THEMES.find((t) => t.id === id) || THEMES[0];

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ShieldAlert, Loader2, CheckCircle2, XCircle, RefreshCw, Trash2, EyeOff, Eye, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { relativeDate } from "@/lib/format";

export default function Admin() {
  const [gate, setGate] = useState("checking");
  const [health, setHealth] = useState(null);
  const [reports, setReports] = useState([]);
  const [videos, setVideos] = useState([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const [healthResponse, reportPage, videoPage, userList] = await Promise.allSettled([
        base44.functions.invoke("health", {}),
        base44.entities.Report.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 40 }),
        base44.entities.Video.filter({}, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 40 }),
        base44.entities.User.list(),
      ]);

      if (healthResponse.status === "fulfilled") setHealth(healthResponse.value?.data || null);
      if (reportPage.status === "fulfilled") setReports(reportPage.value.items || []);
      if (videoPage.status === "fulfilled") setVideos(videoPage.value.items || []);
      if (userList.status === "fulfilled") {
        const list = Array.isArray(userList.value) ? userList.value : userList.value?.items || [];
        setUsers(list);
      }
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    base44.auth
      .me()
      .then((user) => {
        if (user?.role === "admin") {
          setGate("admin");
          load();
        } else {
          setGate("denied");
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setGate("anonymous"));
  }, [load]);

  const resolveReport = async (report, status) => {
    try {
      const updated = await base44.entities.Report.update(report.id, { status });
      setReports((prev) => prev.map((row) => (row.id === report.id ? { ...row, ...updated } : row)));
    } catch (error) {
      setNote(error.message || "Could not update the report.");
    }
  };

  const hideVideo = async (video) => {
    const nextStatus = video.status === "hidden" ? "published" : "hidden";
    try {
      const updated = await base44.entities.Video.update(video.id, { status: nextStatus });
      setVideos((prev) => prev.map((row) => (row.id === video.id ? { ...row, ...updated } : row)));
    } catch (error) {
      setNote(error.message || "Could not update the video.");
    }
  };

  const removeVideo = async (video) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete â€œ${video.title}â€ permanently?`)) return;
    try {
      await base44.entities.Video.delete(video.id);
      setVideos((prev) => prev.filter((row) => row.id !== video.id));
    } catch (error) {
      setNote(error.message || "Could not delete the video.");
    }
  };

  if (gate === "checking") {
    return (
      <div className="grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center">
        <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-primary" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (gate !== "admin") {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-24">
        <EmptyState
          icon={ShieldAlert}
          title="Administrator access only"
          body={
            gate === "denied"
              ? "This profile is signed into the workspace but is not an administrator. Ask a workspace admin for the admin role."
              : "Sign in to the workspace with an administrator account to open the moderation console. Server rules also block non-admins from reading the report queue."
          }
          actionLabel="Back to the theatre"
          actionTo="/"
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Admin console"
        title="Moderation & connections"
        description="Reports are readable only by workspace administrators â€” the rule is enforced by the database, not by hiding buttons."
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={load}
          disabled={busy}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {busy ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />} Refresh
        </button>
      </PageHeader>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] space-y-6 px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        {note && <p className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm">{note}</p>}

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Service connections</h2>
          {!health ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Checking providersâ€¦</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-4 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
              {Object.entries(health.checks || {}).map(([key, check]) => (
                <div key={key} className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-background/40 p-3">
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
                    {check.ok ? (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : (
                      <XCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-destructive" aria-hidden="true" />
                    )}
                    {check.label}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                    {check.ok ? "Connected" : check.error || "Not configured"}
                  </p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            Secret values are never returned to the browser â€” only presence and status.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Report queue</h2>
          {reports.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing reported. The queue is clear.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
              {reports.map((report) => (
                <div key={report.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-background/40 p-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="text-sm text-foreground">
                      <span className="text-primary">{report.reason}</span> Â· {report.target_type}: {report.target_label || report.target_id}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                      {report.status} Â· {relativeDate(report.created_date)}
                      {report.details ? ` Â· ${report.details}` : ""}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => resolveReport(report, "removed")}
                      className="rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
                    >
                      Mark removed
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => resolveReport(report, "dismissed")}
                      className="rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] transition hover:text-muted-foreground"
                    >
                      Dismiss
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Video library</h2>
          {videos.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No videos published yet.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
              {videos.map((video) => (
                <div key={video.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-background/40 p-3">
                  <span className="h-12 w-20 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md bg-muted">
                    {video.thumbnail_url ? <img src={video.thumbnail_url} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" /> : null}
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="line-clamp-1 text-sm text-foreground">{video.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                      {video.status} Â· {video.creator_name || "unknown"} Â· {video.views || 0} views
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => hideVideo(video)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
                  >
                    {video.status === "hidden" ? <Eye className="h-3 w-3" /> : <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" />}
                    {video.status === "hidden" ? "Publish" : "Hide"}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => removeVideo(video)}
                    aria-label={`Delete ${video.title}`}
                    className="text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-destructive"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-xl">
            <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" /> Workspace users
          </h2>
          {users.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Only administrators can list app users. Invite people from the dashboard's App Users page.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-border/40">
              {users.map((user) => (
                <li key={user.id} className="flex items-center justify-between py-2.5 text-sm">
                  <span className="text-foreground">{user.full_name || user.email}</span>
                  <span className="rounded-full border border-border/60 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {user.role}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/AnimeDetail.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Star, Heart, Bookmark, Loader2, Tv, Clock, CalendarDays, Building2, ExternalLink, Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Image } from "@/components/ui/image";
import AnimeRow from "@/components/anime/AnimeRow";
import AnimeStreamLink from "@/components/anime/AnimeStreamLink";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { useAnimeDetail } from "@/hooks/useAnime";
import { useWatchlist } from "@/hooks/useWatchlist";
import { recordAnimeView } from "@/lib/library";
import { fetchMalRating } from "@/lib/animeApi";
import { seasonLabel } from "@/lib/format";

export default function AnimeDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { item, loading, error, reload } = useAnimeDetail(id);
  const watchlist = useWatchlist("watchlist");
  const favorites = useWatchlist("favorite");
  const [mal, setMal] = useState(null);
  const [malChecked, setMalChecked] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!item) return;
    recordAnimeView(item);
    let alive = true;
    setMalChecked(false);
    fetchMalRating(item.titleRomaji || item.display)
      .then((rating) => {
        if (alive) {
          setMal(rating);
          setMalChecked(true);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => alive && setMalChecked(true));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [item]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center">
        <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-primary" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !item) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-24">
        <EmptyState
          title="This title could not be opened"
          body={error || "AniList did not return this entry."}
          actionLabel="Try again"
          onAction={reload}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const savedHere = watchlist.isSaved(item.id);
  const favorited = favorites.isSaved(item.id);

  return (
    <article className="pb-16">
      <div className="relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 h-[420px] overflow-hidden">
          <Image src={item.banner || item.cover} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-40" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background via-background/80 to-background/40" />
        </div>

        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
          <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
            <div className="relative mx-auto w-[200px] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/60 shadow-2xl sm:w-[240px] lg:mx-0 lg:w-full">
              <Image src={item.cover} alt={item.display} className="aspect-[2/3] w-full object-cover" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-primary">
                {item.format?.replace(/_/g, " ") || "Anime"} Â· {seasonLabel(item.season, item.seasonYear)}
              </p>
              <h1 className="mt-3 font-display text-4xl font-medium leading-[1.05] tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">
                {item.display}
              </h1>
              {item.titleRomaji && item.titleRomaji !== item.display && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.titleRomaji}</p>
              )}

              <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                {item.score ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary/15 px-3 py-1.5 text-primary">
                    <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {(item.score / 10).toFixed(1)} AniList
                  </span>
                ) : null}
                {mal?.score ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5">
                    <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {mal.score.toFixed(2)} MAL
                  </span>
                ) : malChecked ? (
                  <span className="rounded-full border border-border/50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    MAL rating unavailable
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="rounded-full border border-border/50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    Checking MALâ€¦
                  </span>
                )}
                {item.episodes ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5">
                    <Tv className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> {item.episodes} episodes
                  </span>
                ) : null}
                {item.duration ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5">
                    <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> {item.duration} min
                  </span>
                ) : null}
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-3 py-1.5">
                  <CalendarDays className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> {item.status?.replace(/_/g, " ").toLowerCase()}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <AnimeStreamLink anime={item} />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => watchlist.toggle(item)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-5 py-3 text-sm transition ${
                    savedHere ? "border-primary/70 bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border/80 hover:border-primary/50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Bookmark className={`h-4 w-4 ${savedHere ? "fill-primary" : ""}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                  {savedHere ? "In watchlist" : "Watchlist"}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => favorites.toggle(item)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-5 py-3 text-sm transition ${
                    favorited ? "border-primary/70 bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border/80 hover:border-primary/50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Heart className={`h-4 w-4 ${favorited ? "fill-primary" : ""}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                  {favorited ? "Favorited" : "Favorite"}
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-7 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground">Synopsis</h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-foreground/85">
                    {item.description || "No synopsis has been published for this title yet."}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <dl className="space-y-3 text-sm">
                  {item.genres?.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground">Genres</dt>
                      <dd className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {item.genres.map((genre) => (
                          <Link
                            key={genre}
                            to={`/browse?genre=${encodeURIComponent(genre)}`}
                            className="rounded-full border border-border/70 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
                          >
                            {genre}
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {item.studios?.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground">Studio</dt>
                      <dd className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Building2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {item.studios.join(", ")}
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {item.nextEpisode && (
                    <div>
                      <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground">Next episode</dt>
                      <dd className="mt-1">
                        Episode {item.nextEpisode.episode} in{" "}
                        {Math.max(1, Math.round(item.nextEpisode.timeUntilAiring / 86400))} days
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {item.trailer?.site === "youtube" && (
                    <div>
                      <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground">Trailer</dt>
                      <dd className="mt-1">
                        <a
                          href={`https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${item.trailer.id}`}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noreferrer noopener"
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-primary hover:underline"
                        >
                          Watch on YouTube <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                        </a>
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </dl>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {item.characters?.length > 0 && (
        <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
          <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-medium">Principal cast</h2>
          <div className="no-scrollbar mt-4 flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
            {item.characters.map((character) => (
              <figure key={character.id} className="w-[104px] shrink-0">
                <Image
                  src={character.image}
                  alt={character.name}
                  className="aspect-[3/4] w-full rounded-xl border border-border/50 object-cover"
                />
                <figcaption className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{character.name}</figcaption>
              </figure>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        <AnimeRow title="If you liked this" subtitle="AniList recommendations" items={item.recommendations || []} viewAll={null} />
        {item.relations?.length > 0 && (
          <AnimeRow title="Same universe" subtitle="Sequels, prequels and spin-offs" items={item.relations} viewAll={null} />
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto mt-6 w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 px-5 py-4">
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">

          </p>
          <Link to="/policy" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary">
            Licensing policy

FILE: src/pages/Browse.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Search, Loader2, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import AnimeCard from "@/components/anime/AnimeCard";
import SkeletonCard from "@/components/anime/SkeletonCard";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { usePaginatedAnime } from "@/hooks/useAnime";

export const GENRES = [
  "Action", "Adventure", "Comedy", "Drama", "Fantasy", "Horror", "Mahou Shoujo", "Mecha",
  "Music", "Mystery", "Psychological", "Romance", "Sci-Fi", "Slice of Life", "Sports", "Supernatural", "Thriller",
];

const SORTS = [
  { id: "popular", label: "Most popular" },
  { id: "trending", label: "Trending" },
  { id: "top", label: "Highest rated" },
  { id: "seasonal", label: "This season" },
];

export default function Browse() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  // `input` is what the user is typing; only the committed (debounced or
  // submitted) term drives the actual request.
  const [input, setInput] = useState(params.get("q") || "");
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState(params.get("genre") || "");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState(params.get("sort") || "popular");
  const debounceRef = useRef(null);
  const committedQuery = (params.get("q") || "").trim();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Don't clobber what the user is mid-typing in the search box.
    if (document.activeElement?.id !== "browse-search") {
      setInput(params.get("q") || "");
    }
    setGenre(params.get("genre") || "");
    setSort(params.get("sort") || "popular");
  }, [params]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    },
    []
  );

  const applyFilters = (next) => {
    const updated = new URLSearchParams(params);
    Object.entries(next).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value) updated.set(key, value);
      else updated.delete(key);
    });
    setParams(updated);
  };

  const commitQuery = (value) => {
    if (debounceRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
      debounceRef.current = null;
    }
    applyFilters({ q: value.trim() });
  };

  // 300ms debounce: typing never fires per-keystroke requests; Enter or the
  // search button commits immediately (empty input clears the search).
  const onSearchInput = (event) => {
    const value = event.target.value;
    setInput(value);
    if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      debounceRef.current = null;
      applyFilters({ q: value.trim() });
    }, 300);
  };

  const clearAllFilters = () => {
    if (debounceRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
      debounceRef.current = null;
    }
    setInput("");
    setParams(new URLSearchParams());
  };

  const body = committedQuery
    ? { action: "search", q: committedQuery, genre: genre || undefined, perPage: 24 }
    : { action: sort, genre: genre || undefined, perPage: 24 };

  const { items, loading, error, hasNext, total, loadMore, retry } = usePaginatedAnime(body);

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Browse"
        title={committedQuery ? `Results for â€œ${committedQuery}â€` : "The whole catalogue"}
        description="Live AniList metadata with scores, genres, formats and episode counts. Filters are applied on the API, not on the page."
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <form
            onSubmit={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              commitQuery(input);
            }}
            className="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/60 px-4"
          >
            <Search className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <label htmlFor="browse-search" className="sr-only">
              Search anime
            </label>
            <input
              id="browse-search"
              value={input}
              onChange={onSearchInput}
              placeholder="Title, franchise, studioâ€¦"
              className="w-56 bg-transparent text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/80"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              aria-label="Search"
              className="grid h-7 w-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground transition hover:brightness-110"
            >
              <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </form>

          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="browse-genre">
            Genre
          </label>
          <select
            id="browse-genre"
            value={genre}
            onChange={(event) => applyFilters({ genre: event.target.value })}
            className="h-11 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/60 px-4 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="">All genres</option>
            {GENRES.map((option) => (
              <option key={option} value={option}>
                {option}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="browse-sort">
            Sort
          </label>
          <select
            id="browse-sort"
            value={sort}
            onChange={(event) => applyFilters({ sort: event.target.value })}
            className="h-11 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/60 px-4 text-sm"
            disabled={Boolean(committedQuery)}
          >
            {SORTS.map((option) => (
              <option key={option.id} value={option.id}>
                {option.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </PageHeader>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
            <SlidersHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
            {loading && items.length === 0
              ? "Querying AniListâ€¦"
              : `${total || items.length} titles${total && items.length < total ? ` Â· showing ${items.length}` : ""}`}
          </span>
          {(committedQuery || genre) && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={clearAllFilters}
              className="transition hover:text-primary"
            >
              Clear filters
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {error ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={Search}
            title="The catalogue could not be reached"
            body={error}
            actionLabel="Retry"
            onAction={retry}
          />
        ) : loading && items.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
            {Array.from({ length: 12 }).map((_, index) => (
              <SkeletonCard key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : items.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            title="No anime found for this search"
            body={`AniList returned no matching titles for â€œ${committedQuery}â€. Try a shorter or differently spelled title${genre ? ", or clear the genre filter" : ""}.`}
            actionLabel="Clear filters"
            onAction={clearAllFilters}
          />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
              {items.map((anime) => (
                <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
            </div>

            {hasNext && (
              <div className="mt-9 flex justify-center">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={loadMore}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border px-6 py-3 text-sm transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />}
                  Load more
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/Category.jsx
================================================================================
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import AnimeCard from "@/components/anime/AnimeCard";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { usePaginatedAnime } from "@/hooks/useAnime";
import { currentSeason } from "@/lib/animeApi";

export const CATEGORY_MAP = {
  action: { title: "Action & Adventure", genre: "Action", description: "Momentum, stakes and spectacle â€” the pulse of the medium." },
  "dark-fantasy": { title: "Dark Fantasy & Demons", genre: "Horror", description: "Cursed kingdoms, hungry shadows and bargains that always cost." },
  magic: { title: "Magic & Supernatural", genre: "Supernatural", description: "Rituals, spirits and powers that rewrite the rules of a world." },
  isekai: { title: "Isekai & Other Worlds", genre: "Fantasy", description: "Portal fantasy: ordinary people, impossible kingdoms." },
  "sci-fi": { title: "Sci-Fi & Futuristic", genre: "Sci-Fi", description: "Neon cities, cold orbits and machines that dream." },
  mystery: { title: "Mystery & Thriller", genre: "Mystery", description: "Every answer opens a darker question." },
  comedy: { title: "Comedy & Slice of Life", genre: "Slice of Life", description: "Small rooms, warm light, ordinary miracles." },
  seasonal: { title: "New & Seasonal", seasonal: true, description: "Everything airing right now, ranked by popularity." },
};

export default function Category() {
  const { slug } = useParams();
  const config = CATEGORY_MAP[slug] || CATEGORY_MAP.action;
  const season = currentSeason();

  const body = config.seasonal
    ? { action: "seasonal", season: season.season, year: season.year, perPage: 24 }
    : { action: "genre", genre: config.genre, perPage: 24 };

  const { items, loading, error, hasNext, total, loadMore } = usePaginatedAnime(body);

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Realm" title={config.title} description={config.description}>
        <span className="rounded-full border border-border/70 px-4 py-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
          {total ? `${total} titles` : "Live query"}
        </span>
      </PageHeader>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        {error ? (
          <EmptyState title="Could not load this realm" body={error} actionLabel="Reload" onAction={() => window.location.reload()} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
              {items.map((anime) => (
                <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
              ))}
              {loading &&
                Array.from({ length: items.length ? 6 : 12 }).map((_, index) => (
                  <div key={`skeleton-${index}`} className="aspect-[2/3] animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" />
                ))}
            </div>

            {hasNext && (
              <div className="mt-9 flex justify-center">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={loadMore}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border px-6 py-3 text-sm transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />}
                  Load more
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/Creator.jsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, Upload, Wand2, Clapperboard, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { usePref, LANGUAGES } from "@/lib/prefs";
import { fetchProviderStatus } from "@/lib/animeApi";

const STYLES = ["cinematic", "manhwa", "painterly", "cel"];

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const map = {
    queued: { label: "Queued", icon: Clock, className: "border-border text-muted-foreground" },
    processing: { label: "Processing", icon: Loader2, className: "border-primary/50 text-primary" },
    completed: { label: "Completed", icon: CheckCircle2, className: "border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-400" },
    failed: { label: "Failed", icon: AlertTriangle, className: "border-destructive/50 text-destructive" },
  };
  const meta = map[status] || map.queued;
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] ${meta.className}`}>
      <meta.icon className={`h-3 w-3 ${status === "processing" ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} aria-hidden="true" />
      {meta.label}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function Creator() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("publish");
  const [language, setLanguage] = usePref("language");
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(null);
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState([]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("Original story");
  const [tags, setTags] = useState("");
  const [videoFile, setVideoFile] = useState(null);
  const [thumbFile, setThumbFile] = useState(null);
  const [licence, setLicence] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [uploadLabel, setUploadLabel] = useState("");
  const [myVideos, setMyVideos] = useState([]);

  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("");
  const [style, setStyle] = useState("cinematic");
  const [aspect, setAspect] = useState("16:9");
  const [rendering, setRendering] = useState(false);

  const loadJobs = useCallback(() => {
    base44.entities.VideoJob.filter({ owner_ref: getProfile().id }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 12 })
      .then((page) => setJobs(page.items || []))
      .catch(() => setJobs([]));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProviderStatus().then(setProvider);
    loadJobs();
    base44.entities.Video.filter({ creator_name: getProfile().name }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 12 })
      .then((page) => setMyVideos(page.items || []))
      .catch(() => setMyVideos([]));
  }, [loadJobs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!jobs.some((job) => job.status === "processing" || job.status === "queued")) return;
    const timer = setInterval(loadJobs, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [jobs, loadJobs]);

  const publish = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (!videoFile) return setError("Choose a video file first.");
    if (!licence) return setError("Confirm you own or are licensed to publish this video.");
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      setUploadLabel("Uploading videoâ€¦");
      const video = await base44.integrations.Core.UploadPublicFile({ file: videoFile });
      let thumbnailUrl = "";
      if (thumbFile) {
        setUploadLabel("Uploading thumbnailâ€¦");
        const thumb = await base44.integrations.Core.UploadPublicFile({ file: thumbFile });
        thumbnailUrl = thumb.file_url;
      }
      setUploadLabel("Publishingâ€¦");
      const created = await base44.entities.Video.create({
        title: title.trim().slice(0, 140),
        description: description.trim().slice(0, 2000),
        video_url: video.file_url,
        thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl,
        category: category.trim().slice(0, 40),
        creator_name: getProfile().name,
        licence: "Creator-declared licence",
        tags: tags.split(",").map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6),
        status: "published",
        views: 0,
      });
      setMyVideos((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
      setMessage("Published. It is now listed in the theatre.");
      setTitle("");
      setDescription("");
      setTags("");
      setVideoFile(null);
      setThumbFile(null);
      setLicence(false);
    } catch (uploadError) {
      setError(uploadError.message || "The upload failed.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      setUploadLabel("");
    }
  };

  const render = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    if (prompt.trim().length < 12) return setError("Describe the shot in at least a sentence.");
    setRendering(true);
    try {
      const response = await base44.functions.invoke("videoJob", {
        prompt: prompt.trim(),
        language,
        style,
        aspect_ratio: aspect,
        owner_ref: getProfile().id,
      });
      const data = response?.data;
      if (data?.job) setJobs((prev) => [data.job, ...prev.filter((job) => job.id !== data.job.id)]);
      if (data?.ok) setMessage("Render complete â€” the clip is ready below.");
      else setError(data?.error || "The render could not be completed.");
      loadJobs();
    } catch (renderError) {
      setError(renderError.message || "The render service is unreachable.");
    } finally {
      setRendering(false);
    }
  };

  const publishJob = async (job) => {
    try {
      const created = await base44.entities.Video.create({
        title: job.prompt.slice(0, 120),
        description: `AI-rendered from a PRI-ANIME atelier prompt.\n\n${job.prompt}`,
        video_url: job.video_url,
        category: "AI original",
        creator_name: getProfile().name,
        licence: "AI-generated â€” PRI-ANIME atelier",
        status: "published",
        views: 0,
        tags: [job.style, job.language],
      });
      setMyVideos((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
      setMessage("AI clip published to the theatre.");
    } catch (publishError) {
      setError(publishError.message || "Could not publish the clip.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Creator studio"
        title="Publish licensed work"
        description="Upload videos you own, or send a scene through the AI render pipeline. Everything is created from environment configuration â€” no keys touch the browser."
      >
        <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-full border border-border/70 p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab("publish")}
            className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs transition ${tab === "publish" ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}
          >
            Upload
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab("render")}
            className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs transition ${tab === "render" ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}
          >
            AI render
          </button>
        </div>
      </PageHeader>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 px-5 py-4 text-sm">
          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 ${provider?.configured ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
            <span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${provider?.configured ? "bg-emerald-400" : "bg-muted-foreground"}`} />
            {provider?.configured ? `${provider.providerLabel} connected` : "No external video provider connected"}
          </span>
          {!provider?.configured && (
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Playback uses the uploaded file URLs. Set VIDEO_PROVIDER, VIDEO_API_URL and VIDEO_API_KEY to enable a
              provider.
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {message && (
          <p className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-primary">{message}</p>
        )}
        {error && (
          <p className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm">{error}</p>
        )}

        {tab === "publish" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_1fr]">
            <form onSubmit={publish} className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-xl">
                <Clapperboard className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" /> New screening
              </h2>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="video-title" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Title
                </label>
                <input
                  id="video-title"
                  value={title}
                  onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
                  required
                  maxLength={140}
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  placeholder="Episode 1 â€” The gate at midnight"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="video-description" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Description
                </label>
                <textarea
                  id="video-description"
                  value={description}
                  onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
                  rows={3}
                  maxLength={2000}
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="video-category" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Category
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="video-category"
                    value={category}
                    onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
                    className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="video-tags" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Tags
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="video-tags"
                    value={tags}
                    onChange={(event) => setTags(event.target.value)}
                    className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                    placeholder="fantasy, pilot"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="video-file" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Video file
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="video-file"
                    type="file"
                    accept="video/*"
                    onChange={(event) => setVideoFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)}
                    className="mt-2 w-full text-xs text-muted-foreground file:mr-3 file:rounded-full file:border-0 file:bg-primary/15 file:px-3 file:py-2 file:text-xs file:text-primary"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="video-thumb" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                    Thumbnail
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="video-thumb"
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    onChange={(event) => setThumbFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)}
                    className="mt-2 w-full text-xs text-muted-foreground file:mr-3 file:rounded-full file:border-0 file:bg-primary/15 file:px-3 file:py-2 file:text-xs file:text-primary"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                Video and images are stored publicly â€” anyone with the link can open them. Upload only work you own.
              </p>

              <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={licence}
                  onChange={(event) => setLicence(event.target.checked)}
                  className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
                />
                I own this video or hold distribution rights, and it follows the content policy.
              </label>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={uploading}

                    <option value="9:16">9:16</option>

                  </label>

                      {job.error && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-destructive">{job.error}</p>}
                      {job.status === "completed" && job.video_url && (

                              Download
                            </a>

                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/ForgotPassword.jsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Mail, ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPasswordRequest(email);
    } catch {
      // Always show success regardless
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setSent(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Mail}
      title="Reset password"
      subtitle="We'll send you a link to reset it"
      footer={
        <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" />Back to log in
        </Link>
      }
    >
      {sent ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          If an account exists with that email, you'll receive a password reset link shortly.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
              <Input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                autoFocus
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-10 h-12"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                Sending...
              </>
            ) : (
              "Send reset link"
            )}
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
