FILE: src/pages/Library.jsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Bookmark, Heart, History, BookOpen, Trash2, Copy } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import AnimeCard from "@/components/anime/AnimeCard";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { listSaved, listProgress, clearProgress } from "@/lib/library";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { percent, relativeDate } from "@/lib/format";

const TABS = [
  { id: "watchlist", label: "Watchlist", icon: Bookmark },
  { id: "favorite", label: "Favorites", icon: Heart },
  { id: "continue", label: "Continue", icon: History },
  { id: "recent", label: "Recently viewed", icon: History },
  { id: "stories", label: "Scripts", icon: BookOpen },
];

export default function Library() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("watchlist");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState({ watchlist: [], favorite: [] });
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState({ video: [], anime: [] });
  const [stories, setStories] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const [watchlist, favorite, videos, anime, storyPage] = await Promise.all([
      listSaved("watchlist").catch(() => []),
      listSaved("favorite").catch(() => []),
      listProgress("video").catch(() => []),
      listProgress("anime").catch(() => []),
      base44.entities.Story.filter({ owner_ref: getProfile().id }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 40 }).catch(() => ({ items: [] })),
    ]);
    setSaved({ watchlist, favorite });
    setProgress({ video: videos, anime });
    setStories(storyPage.items || []);
    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const removeProgress = async (id) => {
    await clearProgress(id);
    setProgress((prev) => ({
      video: prev.video.filter((row) => row.id !== id),
      anime: prev.anime.filter((row) => row.id !== id),
    }));
  };

  const removeStory = async (id) => {
    await base44.entities.Story.delete(id);
    setStories((prev) => prev.filter((story) => story.id !== id));
  };

  const toCard = (row) => ({
    id: row.anime_id,
    display: row.title,
    cover: row.cover,
    banner: row.banner,
    score: row.score,
    episodes: row.episodes,
    format: row.format,
    genres: row.genres || [],
    seasonYear: null,
  });

  const grid = saved[tab] || [];
  const continueRows = tab === "continue" ? progress.video : tab === "recent" ? progress.anime : [];

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Your library"
        title="Everything you keep"
        description="Saved titles, playback history and scripts live on this device profile â€” no account required."
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        <div className="no-scrollbar flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto border-b border-border/50 pb-2" role="tablist">
          {TABS.map((option) => (
            <button
              key={option.id}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={tab === option.id}
              onClick={() => setTab(option.id)}
              className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs transition ${
                tab === option.id ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
            >
              <option.icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              {option.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-6">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="aspect-[2/3] animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : tab === "watchlist" || tab === "favorite" ? (
            grid.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                icon={tab === "watchlist" ? Bookmark : Heart}
                title={tab === "watchlist" ? "Your watchlist is empty" : "No favorites yet"}
                body="Tap the bookmark on any anime page and it will be waiting here."
                actionLabel="Find something to keep"
                actionTo="/browse"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
                {grid.map((row) => (
                  <AnimeCard key={row.id} anime={toCard(row)} />
                ))}
              </div>
            )
          ) : tab === "stories" ? (
            stories.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                icon={BookOpen}
                title="No saved scripts"
                body="Generate a story in the AI atelier and save it â€” it will appear here."
                actionLabel="Open the atelier"
                actionTo="/studio"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2">
                {stories.map((story) => (
                  <article key={story.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-border/50 bg-card/40 p-4">
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                      {story.kind} Â· {story.language} Â· {relativeDate(story.created_date)}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className="mt-1.5 font-display text-lg leading-snug">{story.title}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-4 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-muted-foreground">{story.script}</p>
                    <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(story.script)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary"
                      >
                        <Copy className="h-3 w-3" /> Copy
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => removeStory(story.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-destructive"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" /> Delete
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            )
          ) : continueRows.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={History}
              title={tab === "continue" ? "Nothing in progress" : "No viewing history yet"}
              body="Play something in the theatre and your position will be remembered here."
              actionLabel="Open the theatre"
              actionTo="/watch"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-2">
              {continueRows.map((row) => (
                <div key={row.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card/40 p-3">
                  <Link
                    to={tab === "continue" ? `/watch/${row.ref_id}` : `/anime/${row.ref_id}`}
                    className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3"
                  >
                    <span className="h-14 w-24 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md bg-muted">
                      {row.cover ? <img src={row.cover} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" /> : null}
                    </span>
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span className="line-clamp-1 block text-sm text-foreground">{row.title}</span>
                      <span className="mt-0.5 block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
                        {row.episode ? `Episode ${row.episode} Â· ` : ""}
                        {row.duration ? `${Math.round(percent(row.position, row.duration))}% watched` : "Viewed"}
                      </span>
                      <span className="mt-1 block h-0.5 w-32 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/15">
                        <span className="block h-full bg-primary" style={{ width: `${percent(row.position, row.duration)}%` }} />
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </Link>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => removeProgress(row.id)}
                    aria-label="Remove from history"
                    className="text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-destructive"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/Login.jsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { LogIn, Mail, Lock, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";
import { safeReturnTo } from "@/lib/authReturnTo";

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  // Post-login destination (e.g. the MCP OAuth consent page sends users here
  // with returnTo so the grant flow can resume). Same-origin paths only.
  const returnTo = safeReturnTo();

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.loginViaEmailPassword(email, password);
      window.location.href = returnTo;
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Invalid email or password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", returnTo);
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={LogIn}
      title="Welcome back"
      subtitle="Log in to your account"
      footer={
        <>
          Don't have an account?{" "}
          <Link
            to={"/register" + (returnTo !== "/" ? "?returnTo=" + encodeURIComponent(returnTo) : "")}
            className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline"
          >
            Create one
          </Link>
        </>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continue with Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">or</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
            <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline">
              Forgot password?
            </Link>
          </div>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Logging in...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Log in"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/OAuthConsent.jsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { appParams } from "@/lib/app-params";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

const OPERATION_VERBS = { read: "view", create: "create", update: "edit", delete: "delete" };
const COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS = 6;

// Entity tools arrive one per operation; show each entity once, naming only the
// operations it actually grants.
function groupTools(tools) {
  const operationsByEntity = new Map();
  const agents = [];
  const actions = [];
  for (const tool of tools) {
    if (tool.kind === "entity" && tool.entity) {
      if (!operationsByEntity.has(tool.entity)) operationsByEntity.set(tool.entity, []);
      operationsByEntity.get(tool.entity).push(tool.operation);
    } else if (tool.kind === "agent") {
      agents.push(tool);
    } else {
      actions.push(tool);
    }
  }
  const data = [...operationsByEntity].map(([entity, operations]) => ({
    entity,
    operations: Object.keys(OPERATION_VERBS).filter((op) => operations.includes(op)),
  }));
  return { data, agents, actions };
}

const humanize = (name) =>
  name.split(/[_-]+/).filter(Boolean).map((word) => word[0].toUpperCase() + word.slice(1)).join(" ") || name;

// "View, create and delete records" â€” delete is emphasized so it doesn't hide
// among harmless verbs.
function OperationSummary({ operations }) {
  return (
    <>
      {operations.map((op, i) => {
        const verb = OPERATION_VERBS[op];
        const separator = i === 0 ? "" : i === operations.length - 1 ? " and " : ", ";
        return (
          <React.Fragment key={op}>
            {separator}
            <span className={op === "delete" ? "font-medium text-destructive" : undefined}>
              {i === 0 ? verb[0].toUpperCase() + verb.slice(1) : verb}
            </span>
          </React.Fragment>
        );
      })}{" "}
      records
    </>
  );
}

function ToolSection({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <section>
      <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground mb-2">{title}</h3>
      <ul className="divide-y divide-border rounded-lg border border-border">{children}</ul>
    </section>
  );
}

function ToolRow({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 px-3 py-2">
      <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{title}</span>
      {children && <span className="text-muted-foreground">{children}</span>}
    </li>
  );
}

// App-side OAuth consent page for the app's MCP server. The platform redirects
// AI clients here (see base44/mcp/config.json `consent_path`) with an opaque
// `ctx` handle â€” the authorization request itself lives on the server. This page
// gates on the app-user session, fetches the display info for that handle, shows
// the categories of access being granted, and posts the approve/deny decision.
// Do not change the fetch calls, headers, or the `ctx` handle handling â€” styling
// and copy are safe to edit.
export default function OAuthConsent() {
  const ctx = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("ctx");
  const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [decided, setDecided] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [reconnect, setReconnect] = useState("");
  const [showAllData, setShowAllData] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      let redirecting = false;
      try {
        if (!ctx) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        // Resolve the handle first: a dead handle must never render
        // approve/deny, and the response carries the app's configured login
        // route for the signed-out redirect below. Send the session (cookie +
        // bearer token) so the server can list the granted tools for a
        // signed-in user â€” the same auth the approve/deny call sends; without
        // it the display request is anonymous and shows no tools.
        const infoHeaders = {};
        if (appParams.token) infoHeaders.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
        const res = await fetch(
          `/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/consent-info?handle=${encodeURIComponent(ctx)}`,
          { credentials: "include", headers: infoHeaders },
        );
        if (!res.ok) {
          setError("This authorization link is invalid or has expired.");
          return;
        }
        const data = await res.json();
        // Gate on the server's auth result, NOT base44.auth.isAuthenticated():
        // the SDK check runs the bearer path, so a cookie-only session (platform
        // login/SSO, or a private app with a stale localStorage token) would read
        // as signed-out and redirect â€” even though /consent-info just
        // authenticated this same request via its cookie fallback. data.authenticated
        // keeps the redirect decision in agreement with what the server returned.
        if (!data.authenticated) {
          // The short handle rides back in returnTo; login_path is
          // owner-configured and validated server-side as a same-origin path.
          // Send from_url too: a custom-auth app coerced to platform auth (e.g.
          // public_without_login under workspace SSO) serves the platform login,
          // which honors from_url rather than returnTo. Rebuild the query from
          // `ctx` alone â€” never forward window.location.search raw: the platform
          // resume returns from_url verbatim, so crafted extras on the consent
          // link (app_base_url, access_token, â€¦) would ride through the login
          // round-trip and app-params.js would persist them into the freshly
          // authenticated session.
          const returnTo =
            window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          redirecting = true; // keep the spinner while the browser navigates
          window.location.href =
            (data.login_path || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        setInfo(data);
      } catch (e) {
        setError("Could not load this authorization request. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!redirecting) setChecking(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [ctx]);

  const respond = async (action) => {
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
      // Cookie-backed sessions carry no token; sending "Bearer null" would
      // shadow the valid cookie, so add the header only when a token exists.
      if (appParams.token) headers.Authorization = "Bearer " + appParams.token;
      const res = await fetch(`/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/authorize-grant`, {
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ ctx, action }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        // 401 = the session expired before the (single-use, still-unconsumed)
        // handle was spent; retrying the same controls re-sends the dead session
        // forever. Send the user back through login preserving `ctx` â€” the same
        // redirect the initial signed-out path uses â€” so they can return and
        // approve the still-valid handle.
        if (res.status === 401) {
          const returnTo = window.location.pathname + "?ctx=" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);
          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);
          window.location.href =
            ((info && info.login_path) || "/login") + "?returnTo=" + encoded + "&from_url=" + encoded;
          return;
        }
        // These all come AFTER the single-use handle is atomically consumed
        // (409 tool set changed; 403 host/resource/app mismatch; 404 access
        // gone; 400 malformed/handle already used), so retrying can only 404.
        // Show a terminal reconnect state, not an impossible "try again".
        if ([400, 403, 404, 409].includes(res.status)) {
          let detail = "";
          try { detail = (await res.json()).detail; } catch (_) { /* keep default */ }
          setReconnect(detail || "This authorization can no longer be completed. Reconnect from your AI client to try again.");
          setSubmitting(false);
          return;
        }
        throw new Error("Could not complete authorization. Please try again.");
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      window.location.href = data.redirect_url;
      if (!/^https?:/i.test(data.redirect_url)) {
        // Custom-scheme redirect (native AI clients, e.g. cursor://): browsers
        // may block or not visibly navigate, so show a terminal state instead
        // of an eternal spinner.
        setDecided(action);
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message);
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-6 text-muted-foreground">
          <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
          Loadingâ€¦
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const client = (info && info.client_name) || "An AI client";
  const appName = (info && info.app_name) || "this app";

  if (decided) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={ShieldCheck}
        title={decided === "approve" ? "Access granted" : "Access denied"}
        subtitle={`You can return to ${client} and close this window.`}
      />
    );
  }

  // Terminal: the authorization request is no longer valid (tool set changed +
  // handle consumed). Retrying can't succeed, so show reconnect guidance with
  // no approve/deny controls.
  if (reconnect) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Reconnect required">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {reconnect}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  // No consent details means nothing trustworthy to approve: a failed
  // consent-info load (expired handle, rate limit, transient error) renders
  // the error alone, never the approve/deny controls.
  if (error && !info) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title="Authorize access">
        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  const tools = Array.isArray(info.tools) ? info.tools : [];
  const { data, agents, actions } = groupTools(tools);

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={ShieldCheck}
      title="Authorize access"
      subtitle={`${client} wants to access ${appName} on your behalf`}
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-3">
        {tools.length ? `${client} will be able to:` : "No tools requested"}
      </p>
      {tools.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-4 text-sm mb-6">
          {data.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title={`Data (${data.length})`}>
              {(showAllData ? data : data.slice(0, COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS)).map(({ entity, operations }) => (
                <ToolRow key={entity} title={entity}>
                  <OperationSummary operations={operations} />
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
              {data.length > COLLAPSED_DATA_ROWS && (
                <li className="px-3 py-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
                    onClick={() => setShowAllData((shown) => !shown)}
                  >
                    {showAllData ? "Show less" : `Show all ${data.length}`}
                  </button>
                </li>
              )}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
          {agents.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title="Agents">
              {agents.map((tool) => (
                <ToolRow key={tool.name} title={tool.agent ? humanize(tool.agent) : tool.title || tool.name}>
                  {tool.description}
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
          {actions.length > 0 && (
            <ToolSection title="Actions">
              {actions.map((tool) => (
                <ToolRow key={tool.name} title={tool.title || tool.name}>
                  {tool.description}
                </ToolRow>
              ))}
            </ToolSection>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("deny")}
        >
          Deny
        </Button>
        <Button
          className="flex-1 h-12 font-medium"
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={() => respond("approve")}
        >
          {submitting ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Approve
        </Button>
      </div>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/Policy.jsx
================================================================================
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";

const SECTIONS = [
  {
    title: "What this app streams",
    body: "PRI-ANIME plays video that the creator uploaded through the creator studio, or open-licensed demo clips shipped with the app. AniList and MyAnimeList are metadata providers only â€” they do not host or licence episodes, and this app never scrapes streaming sites or stores pirate links.",
  },
  {
    title: "Metadata licence",
    body: "Titles, artwork, genres and scores are fetched from the official AniList GraphQL API at runtime. MyAnimeList ratings appear only when that provider returns real data with valid credentials. Anime artwork remains the property of its licence holders and is used here for identification only.",
  },
  {
    title: "Uploads & moderation",
    body: "Every reel and screening stores a creator-declared licence. Anyone can report a reel, a video or a comment; reports land in the admin moderation queue, which the database only exposes to workspace administrators. Admins can hide or delete content.",
  },
  {
    title: "Configuration",
    body: "Secrets live in environment variables on the server. Nothing private is written to browser storage, HTML or public API responses. Video provider checks return status only â€” never a key.",
  },
];

const ENV_VARS = [
  ["ANILIST_API_URL", "https://graphql.anilist.co", "Public GraphQL endpoint. No key required."],
  ["MAL_CLIENT_ID", "â€”", "From the MyAnimeList API panel. Required for MAL ratings."],
  ["MAL_CLIENT_SECRET", "â€”", "Only needed for refresh-token flows."],
  ["MAL_ACCESS_TOKEN", "â€”", "Bearer token from the MAL OAuth flow."],
  ["VIDEO_PROVIDER", "cloudflare | mux | generic", "Selects the adapter."],
  ["VIDEO_API_URL", "â€”", "Provider API base, e.g. Cloudflare account stream URL."],

export default function Policy() {
  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader

      />

            Set these in the workspace environment settings â€” never in frontend files. Missing values are reported as
            â€œnot configuredâ€ rather than faked.

                  <th className="py-2 pr-4">Variable</th>
                  <th className="py-2 pr-4">Example</th>
                  <th className="py-2">Purpose</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

                {ENV_VARS.map(([name, example, purpose]) => (
                  <tr key={name} className="border-b border-border/30 last:border-0">
                    <td className="py-2.5 pr-4 font-mono text-xs text-primary">{name}</td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 pr-4 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{example}</td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 text-muted-foreground">{purpose}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>

{`cd C:\\path\\to\\pri-anime
npm install
npm run dev
# open http://localhost:5173

# production checks
npm run lint
npm run build`}
          </pre>
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            In VS Code: open the folder, then Terminal â†’ New Terminal, and paste the commands above.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Going live on your own domain</h2>
          <ol className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {[
              "Publish the app, then connect your domain from the dashboard's Domains page and point DNS at it.",
              "Serve over HTTPS only; HSTS protects signed playback URLs.",
              "Add the production origin to CORS allow-lists for every provider you call from the browser.",
              "Store all provider keys as environment variables â€” rotate them if a key ever appears in client code.",
              "Move metadata caching to a shared cache (Redis or edge KV) before traffic grows; the in-process cache is per instance.",

            <li>â€¢ MyAnimeList ratings need real credentials; until then the app says â€œunavailableâ€.</li>
            <li>â€¢ AI video rendering depends on the workspace plan and the video model's limits.</li>
            <li>â€¢ No claim is made about extreme concurrency â€” that needs load testing and shared infrastructure.</li>

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/Reels.jsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, Plus, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import ReelCard from "@/components/reels/ReelCard";
import ReelComments from "@/components/reels/ReelComments";
import ReelUploadDialog from "@/components/reels/ReelUploadDialog";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";

const PAGE_SIZE = 4;

export default function Reels() {
  const [reels, setReels] = useState([]);
  const [cursor, setCursor] = useState(null);
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState(null);
  const [commenting, setCommenting] = useState(null);
  const [uploadOpen, setUploadOpen] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState({ likes: {}, bookmarks: {} });
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const feedRef = useRef(null);
  const sentinelRef = useRef(null);
  const loadingRef = useRef(false);
  const viewedRef = useRef(new Set());

  const loadMore = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loadingRef.current || !hasMore) return;
    loadingRef.current = true;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const options = { sort: "-created_date", limit: PAGE_SIZE };
      if (cursor) options.cursor = cursor;
      const page = await base44.entities.Reel.filter({ status: "published" }, options);
      setReels((prev) => {
        const known = new Set(prev.map((row) => row.id));
        return [...prev, ...(page.items || []).filter((row) => !known.has(row.id))];
      });
      setCursor(page.next_cursor || null);
      setHasMore(Boolean(page.has_more));
      setError("");
    } catch (loadError) {
      setError(loadError.message || "The reel feed could not be loaded.");
      setHasMore(false);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      loadingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [cursor, hasMore]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadMore();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    base44.entities.ReelLike.filter({ owner_ref: getProfile().id }, { limit: 300 })
      .then((page) => {
        const likes = {};
        const bookmarks = {};
        (page.items || []).forEach((row) => {
          if (row.kind === "bookmark") bookmarks[row.reel_id] = row.id;
          else likes[row.reel_id] = row.id;
        });
        setSaved({ likes, bookmarks });
      })
      .catch(() => setSaved({ likes: {}, bookmarks: {} }));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = feedRef.current;
    if (!root) return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio >= 0.55) {
            setActiveId(entry.target.getAttribute("data-reel"));
          }
        });
      },
      { root, threshold: [0.55] }
    );
    root.querySelectorAll("[data-reel]").forEach((node) => observer.observe(node));
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [reels.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const sentinel = sentinelRef.current;
    const root = feedRef.current;
    if (!sentinel || !root) return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries[0]?.isIntersecting) loadMore();
      },
      { root, rootMargin: "600px" }
    );
    observer.observe(sentinel);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [loadMore]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeId || viewedRef.current.has(activeId)) return;
    viewedRef.current.add(activeId);
    const reel = reels.find((row) => row.id === activeId);
    if (!reel) return;
    base44.entities.Reel.update(reel.id, { views: (reel.views || 0) + 1 }).catch(() => {});
  }, [activeId, reels]);

  const toggleReaction = async (reel, kind) => {
    const store = kind === "bookmark" ? saved.bookmarks : saved.likes;
    const existingId = store[reel.id];
    try {
      if (existingId) {
        await base44.entities.ReelLike.delete(existingId);
        setSaved((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev[kind === "bookmark" ? "bookmarks" : "likes"] };
          delete next[reel.id];
          return { ...prev, [kind === "bookmark" ? "bookmarks" : "likes"]: next };
        });
        if (kind === "like") {
          await base44.entities.Reel.update(reel.id, { likes: Math.max(0, (reel.likes || 0) - 1) });
          setReels((prev) => prev.map((row) => (row.id === reel.id ? { ...row, likes: Math.max(0, (row.likes || 0) - 1) } : row)));
        }
      } else {
        const created = await base44.entities.ReelLike.create({
          reel_id: reel.id,
          owner_ref: getProfile().id,
          kind: kind === "bookmark" ? "bookmark" : "like",
        });
        setSaved((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [kind === "bookmark" ? "bookmarks" : "likes"]: { ...prev[kind === "bookmark" ? "bookmarks" : "likes"], [reel.id]: created.id },
        }));
        if (kind === "like") {
          await base44.entities.Reel.update(reel.id, { likes: (reel.likes || 0) + 1 });
          setReels((prev) => prev.map((row) => (row.id === reel.id ? { ...row, likes: (row.likes || 0) + 1 } : row)));
        }
      }
    } catch (reactionError) {
      setMessage(reactionError.message || "That action did not save.");
    }
  };

  const share = async (reel) => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/reels?reel=${reel.id}`;
    try {
      if (navigator.share) await navigator.share({ title: reel.title, url });
      else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        setMessage("Link copied to clipboard.");
      }
    } catch {
      setMessage("Sharing was cancelled.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-4 z-20 flex justify-center">
        <div className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/60 bg-black/50 px-4 py-2 backdrop-blur">
          <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-white/70">Reel vortex</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setUploadOpen(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground"
          >
            <Plus className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" /> Upload
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {message && (
        <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-20 z-20 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-black/75 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-white backdrop-blur">
          {message}
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        ref={feedRef}
        className="h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)] snap-y snap-mandatory overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain bg-black"
        aria-label="Vertical reels feed"
      >
        {reels.map((reel) => (
          <div key={reel.id} data-reel={reel.id}>
            <ReelCard
              reel={reel}
              active={activeId === reel.id}
              liked={Boolean(saved.likes[reel.id])}
              bookmarked={Boolean(saved.bookmarks[reel.id])}
              onLike={(item) => toggleReaction(item, "like")}
              onBookmark={(item) => toggleReaction(item, "bookmark")}
              onComment={(item) => setCommenting(item)}
              onShare={share}
            />
          </div>
        ))}

        {loading && (
          <div className="grid h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)] place-items-center text-white/60">
            <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-primary" />
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && error && (
          <div className="grid h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)] place-items-center px-8 text-center">
            <div>
              <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-6 w-6 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
              <p className="mt-3 font-display text-xl text-white">The feed is quiet</p>
              <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm text-white/60">{error}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setError("");
                  setHasMore(true);
                  loadMore();
                }}
                className="mt-4 rounded-full border border-white/25 px-5 py-2 text-sm text-white transition hover:border-primary hover:text-primary"
              >
                Retry
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && !error && reels.length === 0 && (
          <div className="grid h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)] place-items-center px-8 text-center">
            <div>
              <p className="font-display text-2xl text-white">No reels yet</p>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm text-white/60">
                Be the first â€” upload a clip you own or have a licence for.
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setUploadOpen(true)}
                className="mt-5 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"
              >
                Upload a reel
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div ref={sentinelRef} className="h-1 w-full" />
      </div>

      <ReelComments reel={commenting} open={Boolean(commenting)} onOpenChange={(next) => !next && setCommenting(null)} />

      <ReelUploadDialog
        open={uploadOpen}
        onOpenChange={setUploadOpen}
        onUploaded={() => {
          setReels([]);
          setCursor(null);
          setHasMore(true);
          setUploadOpen(false);
          loadMore();
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/Register.jsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { UserPlus, Mail, Lock, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { InputOTP, InputOTPGroup, InputOTPSlot } from "@/components/ui/input-otp";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";
import GoogleIcon from "@/components/GoogleIcon";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/use-toast";
import { safeReturnTo } from "@/lib/authReturnTo";

export default function Register() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [showOtp, setShowOtp] = useState(false);
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState("");

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.register({ email, password });
      setShowOtp(true);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Registration failed");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await base44.auth.verifyOtp({ email, otpCode });
      if (result?.access_token) {
        base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
      }
      window.location.href = safeReturnTo();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Invalid verification code");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await base44.auth.resendOtp(email);
      toast({
        title: "Code sent",
        description: "Check your email for the new code.",
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Failed to resend code");
    }
  };

  const handleGoogle = () => {
    base44.auth.loginWithProvider("google", safeReturnTo());
  };

  if (showOtp) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={Mail}
        title="Verify your email"
        subtitle={`We sent a code to ${email}`}
      >
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <InputOTP
            maxLength={6}
            value={otpCode}
            onChange={setOtpCode}
            autoFocus
            autoComplete="one-time-code"
          >
            <InputOTPGroup>
              <InputOTPSlot index={0} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={1} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={2} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={3} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={4} />
              <InputOTPSlot index={5} />
            </InputOTPGroup>
          </InputOTP>
        </div>
        <Button
          className="w-full h-12 font-medium"
          onClick={handleVerify}
          disabled={loading || otpCode.length < 6}
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Verifying...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Verify"
          )}
        </Button>
        <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-4">
          Didn't receive the code?{" "}
          <button onClick={handleResend} className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Resend
          </button>
        </p>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={UserPlus}
      title="Create your account"
      subtitle="Sign up to get started"
      footer={
        <>
          Already have an account?{" "}
          <Link
            to={"/login" + (safeReturnTo() !== "/" ? "?returnTo=" + encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo()) : "")}
            className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline"
          >
            Log in
          </Link>
        </>
      }
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        className="w-full h-12 text-sm font-medium mb-6"
        onClick={handleGoogle}
      >
        <GoogleIcon className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
        Continue with Google
      </Button>

      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
          <div className="w-full border-t border-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase">
          <span className="bg-card px-3 text-muted-foreground">or</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirm Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="confirm"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Creating account...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Create account"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/ResetPassword.jsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Lock, Loader2, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ResetPassword() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const resetToken = searchParams.get("token");

  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPassword({ resetToken, newPassword });
      window.location.href = "/login";
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Failed to reset password");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!resetToken) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout
        icon={AlertTriangle}
        title="Invalid reset link"
        subtitle="This password reset link is missing or invalid"
        footer={
          <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
            Request a new link
          </Link>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          The link you used appears to be incomplete. Please request a new password reset email.
        </p>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Lock}
      title="New password"
      subtitle="Enter your new password below"
    >
      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="password">New Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              autoFocus
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              value={newPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Label htmlFor="confirm">Confirm Password</Label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
            <Input
              id="confirm"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              value={confirmPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 h-12"
              required
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
              Resetting...
            </>
          ) : (
            "Reset password"
          )}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Check, RotateCcw, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import { useProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { useTheme } from "@/lib/theme";
import { usePref, LANGUAGES } from "@/lib/prefs";

const TOGGLES = [
  { key: "autoplayReels", label: "Autoplay reels", hint: "Play the next adjacent reel when it scrolls into view." },
  { key: "reelMuted", label: "Start reels muted", hint: "Unmute manually â€” browsers require it anyway." },
  { key: "subtitles", label: "Captions when available", hint: "Turn on subtitle tracks automatically." },
  { key: "reduceMotion", label: "Reduce motion", hint: "Fewer parallax and glow effects." },
  { key: "notifications", label: "Job notifications", hint: "Show render completions in the notification tray." },
  { key: "dataSaver", label: "Data saver", hint: "Load lighter previews where supported." },
];

function PrefToggle({ toggle }) {
  const [value, setValue] = usePref(toggle.key);
  return (
    <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-background/40 p-3">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        checked={Boolean(value)}
        onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.checked)}
        className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 accent-[hsl(var(--primary))]"
      />
      <span>
        <span className="block text-sm text-foreground">{toggle.label}</span>
        <span className="mt-0.5 block text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{toggle.hint}</span>
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

export default function Settings() {
  const [profile, updateProfile] = useProfile();
  const { theme, setTheme, themes } = useTheme();
  const [name, setName] = useState(profile.name);
  const [bio, setBio] = useState(profile.bio || "");
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [language, setLanguage] = usePref("language");

  const save = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    updateProfile({ name: name.trim().slice(0, 40) || "Guest Viewer", bio: bio.trim().slice(0, 160) });
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Settings"
        title="Make it yours"
        description="Theme, language and playback preferences are stored on this device and survive every reload."
      />

      <div className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-[1100px] gap-6 px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:px-10">
        <form onSubmit={save} className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Viewer profile</h2>
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-4">
            <span className="grid h-16 w-16 place-items-center rounded-full border border-primary/40 bg-primary/15 font-display text-2xl text-primary">
              {(name || "G").slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              This profile names your comments and saved scripts. No password, no email â€” it lives in this browser.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5">
            <label htmlFor="profile-name" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
              Display name
            </label>
            <input
              id="profile-name"
              value={name}
              onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
              maxLength={40}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4">
            <label htmlFor="profile-bio" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
              Bio
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="profile-bio"
              value={bio}
              onChange={(event) => setBio(event.target.value)}
              rows={2}
              maxLength={160}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"
          >
            {saved ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : null}
            {saved ? "Saved" : "Save profile"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Visual theme</h2>
          <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {themes.map((option) => (
              <button
                key={option.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setTheme(option.id)}
                aria-pressed={theme === option.id}
                className={`rounded-xl border p-3 text-left transition ${
                  theme === option.id ? "border-primary/70 bg-primary/10" : "border-border/60 hover:border-primary/40"
                }`}
              >
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="mb-2 flex h-7 overflow-hidden rounded-md ring-1 ring-border/60">
                  {option.dots.map((dot) => (
                    <span key={dot} className="flex-1" style={{ backgroundColor: dot }} />
                  ))}
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] font-medium">
                  {option.name}
                  {theme === option.id && <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-primary" />}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5">
            <label htmlFor="pref-language" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
              Story language
            </label>
            <select
              id="pref-language"
              value={language}
              onChange={(event) => setLanguage(event.target.value)}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            >
              {LANGUAGES.map((option) => (
                <option key={option.id} value={option.id}>
                  {option.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-5 lg:col-span-2">
          <h2 className="font-display text-xl">Playback & privacy</h2>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {TOGGLES.map((toggle) => (
              <PrefToggle key={toggle.key} toggle={toggle} />
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border/50 p-3">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
              Clearing local data removes your profile, saved lists and preferences from this browser.
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if (window.confirm("Clear all local PRI-ANIME data on this device?")) {
                  Object.keys(localStorage)
                    .filter((key) => key.startsWith("pri-anime:"))
                    .forEach((key) => localStorage.removeItem(key));
                  window.location.reload();
                }
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-destructive/50 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-destructive transition hover:bg-destructive/10"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Clear local data
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/pages/Studio.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Copy, Loader2, Save, Send, Sparkles, Trash2, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/profile";
import { usePref, LANGUAGES } from "@/lib/prefs";
import { relativeDate } from "@/lib/format";

const MODES = [
  { id: "chat", label: "Recommendations" },
  { id: "story", label: "Story treatment" },
  { id: "episode", label: "Episode outline" },
  { id: "character", label: "Character bible" },
  { id: "dialogue", label: "Scene dialogue" },
];

const STYLES = ["cinematic", "manhwa", "painterly", "cel"];

export default function Studio() {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("chat");
  const [language, setLanguage] = usePref("language");
  const [style, setStyle] = useState("cinematic");
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [stories, setStories] = useState([]);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    base44.entities.Story.filter({ owner_ref: getProfile().id }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 20 })
      .then((page) => setStories(page.items || []))
      .catch(() => setStories([]));
  }, []);

  const send = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const prompt = input.trim();
    if (!prompt || busy) return;
    const nextMessages = [...messages, { role: "user", content: prompt }];
    setMessages(nextMessages);
    setInput("");
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const response = await base44.functions.invoke("aiStudio", {
        mode,
        prompt,
        language,
        style,
        messages: nextMessages,
      });
      const data = response?.data;
      if (data?.ok) {
        setMessages([...nextMessages, { role: "assistant", content: data.text }]);
      } else {
        setError(data?.error || "The atelier did not answer. Try again.");
      }
    } catch (invokeError) {
      setError(invokeError.message || "The AI studio is unreachable right now.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const copy = async (content, id) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(content);
      setCopied(id);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1800);
    } catch {
      setError("Your browser blocked clipboard access.");
    }
  };

  const saveStory = async (content, id) => {
    const asked = [...messages].reverse().find((message) => message.role === "user")?.content || "Untitled";
    try {
      const created = await base44.entities.Story.create({
        owner_ref: getProfile().id,
        title: asked.slice(0, 90),
        kind: mode === "chat" ? "story" : mode,
        language,
        style,
        script: content,
        prompt: asked,
      });
      setStories((prev) => [created, ...prev]);
    } catch (saveError) {
      setError(saveError.message || "Could not save the script.");
    }
  };

  const removeStory = async (id) => {
    try {
      await base44.entities.Story.delete(id);
      setStories((prev) => prev.filter((story) => story.id !== id));
    } catch (deleteError) {
      setError(deleteError.message || "Could not delete the script.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="AI atelier"
        title="Write the next original arc"
        description="Recommendations, world-building, character bibles, episode outlines and scene dialogue â€” written in English or Hindi by PRI-ANIME's AI atelier."
      />

      <div className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-[1600px] gap-6 px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_360px] lg:px-10">
        <section className="flex min-h-[62vh] flex-col rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-border/50 px-4 py-3">
            {MODES.map((option) => (
              <button
                key={option.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setMode(option.id)}
                className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs transition ${
                  mode === option.id ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                }`}
              >
                {option.label}
              </button>
            ))}
            <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
              <label htmlFor="studio-language" className="sr-only">
                Language
              </label>
              <select
                id="studio-language"
                value={language}
                onChange={(event) => setLanguage(event.target.value)}
                className="rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"
              >
                {LANGUAGES.map((option) => (
                  <option key={option.id} value={option.id}>
                    {option.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <label htmlFor="studio-style" className="sr-only">
                Visual style
              </label>
              <select
                id="studio-style"
                value={style}
                onChange={(event) => setStyle(event.target.value)}
                className="rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"
              >
                {STYLES.map((option) => (
                  <option key={option} value={option}>
                    {option}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-5 thin-scrollbar sm:px-6" aria-live="polite">
            {messages.length === 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-background/40 p-5">
                <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
                <p className="mt-3 font-display text-lg">Start with a seed</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  â€œA swordswoman who can only speak the truth hires herself out as a court liar.â€ Try a logline, a
                  character, or ask which anime to watch next.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {messages.map((message, index) => (
              <div
                key={index}
                className={`rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 ${
                  message.role === "user"
                    ? "ml-auto max-w-[85%] border border-primary/30 bg-primary/10"
                    : "max-w-[92%] border border-border/50 bg-background/50"
                }`}
              >
                <p className="mb-1.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
                  {message.role === "user" ? "Director" : "Atelier"}
                </p>
                <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-foreground/90">{message.content}</p>
                {message.role === "assistant" && (
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => copy(message.content, index)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary"
                    >
                      {copied === index ? <Check className="h-3 w-3" /> : <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />} Copy
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => saveStory(message.content, index)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary"
                    >
                      <Save className="h-3 w-3" /> Save script
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}

            {busy && (
              <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-primary" /> The atelier is writingâ€¦
              </p>
            )}
            {error && <p className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm">{error}</p>}
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={send} className="border-t border-border/50 p-3 sm:p-4">
            <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
              <label htmlFor="studio-input" className="sr-only">
                Prompt
              </label>
              <textarea
                id="studio-input"
                value={input}
                onChange={(event) => setInput(event.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(event) => {
                  if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    send(event);
                  }
                }}
                rows={2}
                maxLength={4000}
                placeholder="Describe the story, character or sceneâ€¦"
                className="min-h-[52px] flex-1 resize-none rounded-xl border border-border bg-background px-4 py-3 text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={busy || !input.trim()}
                className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground transition disabled:opacity-40"
                aria-label="Send prompt"
              >
                {busy ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </section>

        <aside>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border/60 pb-3">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg">Saved scripts</h2>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stories.length}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
            {stories.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 px-4 py-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Saved drafts appear here. They stay on this device profile.
              </p>
            ) : (
              stories.map((story) => (
                <div key={story.id} className="rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card/50 p-3">
                  <p className="line-clamp-2 text-sm text-foreground">{story.title}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {story.kind} Â· {story.language} Â· {relativeDate(story.created_date)}
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => copy(story.script, story.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-primary"
                    >
                      <Copy className="h-3 w-3" /> Copy
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeStory(story.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition hover:text-destructive"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" /> Delete
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
