FILE: src/pages/Videos.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Search, Loader2, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import PageHeader from "@/components/common/PageHeader";
import VideoCard from "@/components/video/VideoCard";
import VidstuckSource from "@/components/video/VidstuckSource";
import AkaSource from "@/components/video/AkaSource";
import AnimeScreening from "@/components/video/AnimeScreening";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import { listProgress } from "@/lib/library";
import { percent } from "@/lib/format";

export default function Videos() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const requestedAnime = Number(params.get("anime"));
  const animeId = Number.isSafeInteger(requestedAnime) && requestedAnime > 0 && requestedAnime <= 100000000 ? requestedAnime : null;
  const [videos, setVideos] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState({});
  const [showVidstuck, setShowVidstuck] = useState(() => new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("source") === "vidstuck");
  const [showAka, setShowAka] = useState(() => new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("source") === "aka");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (animeId) return;
    let alive = true;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setLoading(true);
      const filter = { status: "published" };
      if (query.trim()) filter.title = { $regex: query.trim(), $options: "i" };
      base44.entities.Video.filter(filter, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 48 })
        .then((page) => {
          if (!alive) return;
          setVideos(page.items || []);
          setError("");
        })
        .catch((loadError) => alive && setError(loadError.message))
        .finally(() => alive && setLoading(false));
    }, 300);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query, animeId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (animeId) return;
    listProgress("video")
      .then((rows) => {
        const map = {};
        rows.forEach((row) => {
          map[row.ref_id] = percent(row.position, row.duration);
        });
        setProgress(map);
      })
      .catch(() => setProgress({}));
  }, [animeId]);

  if (animeId) return <AnimeScreening key={animeId} animeId={animeId} />;

  return (
    <div className="pb-16">
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow="Theatre"
        title="Long-form screenings"
        description="Full episodes, original shorts and manhwa-style films published by creators â€” played from licensed sources only."
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={showVidstuck}
          onClick={() => setShowVidstuck((visible) => !visible)}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition ${showVidstuck ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border/70 text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"}`}
        >
          Vidstuck source
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={showAka}
          onClick={() => setShowAka((visible) => !visible)}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition ${showAka ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary" : "border-border/70 text-muted-foreground hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"}`}
        >
          Aka source
        </button>
        <Link
          to="/creator"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground"
        >
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" /> Publish
        </Link>
        <div className="flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/60 px-4">
          <Search className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
          <label htmlFor="video-search" className="sr-only">
            Search videos
          </label>
          <input
            id="video-search"
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search the theatreâ€¦"
            className="w-44 bg-transparent text-sm outline-none"
          />
        </div>
      </PageHeader>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
        {showVidstuck && <VidstuckSource />}
        {showAka && <AkaSource />}
        {error ? (
          <EmptyState title="The theatre could not be loaded" body={error} />
        ) : loading ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/40">
                <div className="aspect-video animate-pulse bg-muted" />
                <div className="space-y-2 p-4">
                  <div className="h-3 w-3/4 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
                  <div className="h-2 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted/70" />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : videos.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={Loader2}
            title={query ? "Nothing matched that search" : "No screenings yet"}
            body={query ? "Try a different word." : "Publish the first video from the creator studio."}
            actionLabel={query ? undefined : "Open creator studio"}
            actionTo={query ? undefined : "/creator"}
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {videos.map((video) => (
              <VideoCard key={video.id} video={video} progress={progress[video.id] || 0} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/Watch.jsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Loader2, ListVideo, Flag } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import VideoPlayer from "@/components/video/VideoPlayer";
import EmptyState from "@/components/common/EmptyState";
import ReportDialog from "@/components/common/ReportDialog";
import { saveProgress, listProgress } from "@/lib/library";
import { formatClock, relativeDate } from "@/lib/format";

export default function Watch() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [video, setVideo] = useState(null);
  const [related, setRelated] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [startAt, setStartAt] = useState(0);
  const lastSaved = useRef(0);
  const counted = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setStartAt(0);
    base44.entities.Video.get(id)
      .then(async (row) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setVideo(row);
        setLoading(false);
        const [progressRows, siblings] = await Promise.all([
          listProgress("video").catch(() => []),
          base44.entities.Video.filter({ status: "published" }, { sort: "-created_date", limit: 24 }).catch(() => ({ items: [] })),
        ]);
        if (!alive) return;
        const mine = progressRows.find((entry) => entry.ref_id === String(row.id));
        if (mine?.position) setStartAt(mine.position);
        setRelated((siblings.items || []).filter((item) => item.id !== row.id).slice(0, 6));
        if (!counted.current) {
          counted.current = true;
          base44.entities.Video.update(row.id, { views: (row.views || 0) + 1 }).catch(() => {});
        }
      })
      .catch((loadError) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setError(loadError.message || "This video could not be found.");
        setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [id]);

  const handleProgress = (position, duration) => {
    if (!video) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastSaved.current < 5000) return;
    lastSaved.current = now;
    saveProgress({
      kind: "video",
      ref_id: video.id,
      title: video.title,
      cover: video.thumbnail_url,
      episode: video.episode_number || 1,
      position,
      duration,
      link: `/watch/${video.id}`,
    }).catch(() => {});
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid min-h-[60vh] place-items-center">
        <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-primary" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !video) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-24">
        <EmptyState
          title="This screening is unavailable"
          body={error || "The video may have been unpublished."}
          actionLabel="Back to the theatre"
          actionTo="/watch"
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1600px] px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
      <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <VideoPlayer
            src={video.video_url}
            title={video.title}
            poster={video.thumbnail_url}
            subtitles={video.subtitles_url}
            startAt={startAt}
            onProgress={handleProgress}
          />

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="max-w-2xl">
              <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.26em] text-primary">{video.category || "Screening"}</p>
              <h1 className="mt-2 font-display text-3xl font-medium leading-tight sm:text-4xl">{video.title}</h1>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
                {[video.creator_name, video.licence, relativeDate(video.created_date), video.duration ? formatClock(video.duration) : null]
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .join(" Â· ")}
              </p>
              {video.description && (
                <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-line text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-foreground/85">{video.description}</p>
              )}
              {video.tags?.length > 0 && (
                <p className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {video.tags.map((tag) => (
                    <span key={tag} className="rounded-full border border-border/60 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                      #{tag}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <ReportDialog
              targetType="video"
              targetId={video.id}
              targetLabel={video.title}
              trigger={
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/70 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
                >
                  <Flag className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> Report
                </button>
              }
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-24 lg:self-start">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border/60 pb-3">
            <ListVideo className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" />
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg">Up next</h2>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
            {related.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="py-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No other screenings are published yet.</p>
            ) : (
              related.map((item) => (
                <Link
                  key={item.id}
                  to={`/watch/${item.id}`}
                  className="flex gap-3 rounded-xl border border-transparent p-2 transition hover:border-border/60 hover:bg-card/50"
                >
                  <span className="relative aspect-video w-28 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-muted">
                    {item.thumbnail_url ? (
                      <img src={item.thumbnail_url} alt="" loading="lazy" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                    ) : null}
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0">
                    <span className="line-clamp-2 block text-[13px] leading-snug text-foreground">{item.title}</span>
                    <span className="mt-1 block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
                      {item.category || "Screening"}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </Link>
              ))
            )}
          </div>

          <Link
            to="/watch"
            className="mt-5 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-border/70 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition hover:border-primary/60 hover:text-primary"
          >
            All screenings
          </Link>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function createPageUrl(pageName: string) {
    return '/' + pageName.replace(/ /g, '-');
}

FILE: tests/searchUtils.test.mjs
================================================================================
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { searchVariants, isRetryableFetchError, withRetries } from "../src/lib/searchUtils.js";

test("trims leading and trailing spaces from a search term", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("  Wind Breaker  "), ["Wind Breaker"]);
});

test("collapses internal whitespace runs", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("wind    breaker"), ["wind breaker"]);
});

test("handles uppercase and lowercase consistently (case-insensitive API)", () => {
  const lower = searchVariants("wind breaker");
  const upper = searchVariants("WIND BREAKER");
  const mixed = searchVariants("WiNd BrEaKeR");
  assert.equal(lower.length, upper.length);
  assert.equal(lower.length, mixed.length);
  assert.deepEqual(lower, ["wind breaker"]);
  assert.deepEqual(upper, ["WIND BREAKER"]);
});

test("adds a punctuation-stripped variant when punctuation is present", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("Wind Breaker!!"), ["Wind Breaker!!", "Wind Breaker"]);
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("Spy x Family (TV)"), ["Spy x Family (TV)", "Spy x Family TV"]);
});

test("adds an article-dropped variant when articles are present", () => {
  const variants = searchVariants("The Eminence in Shadow");
  assert.equal(variants[0], "The Eminence in Shadow");
  assert.ok(variants.includes("Eminence in Shadow"));
});

test("does not duplicate identical variants", () => {
  assert.equal(searchVariants("Wind Breaker").length, 1);
});

test("empty and whitespace-only input produce no variants (no request)", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants(""), []);
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("   "), []);
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants(null), []);
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants(undefined), []);
});

test("network failures (TypeError) and timeouts are retryable", () => {
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(new TypeError("failed to fetch")), true);
  const timeout = new Error("timed out");
  timeout.name = "TimeoutError";
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(timeout), true);
  const abort = new Error("aborted");
  abort.name = "AbortError";
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(abort), true);
});

test("rate limits and server errors are retryable; 4xx are not", () => {
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(Object.assign(new Error("429"), { status: 429 })), true);
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(Object.assign(new Error("502"), { status: 502 })), true);
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(Object.assign(new Error("400"), { status: 400 })), false);
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(Object.assign(new Error("403"), { status: 403 })), false);
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(null), false);
});

test("explicit non-retryable marker wins over network-failure classification", () => {
  const forced = new TypeError("failed to fetch");
  forced.retryable = false;
  assert.equal(isRetryableFetchError(forced), false);
});

test("withRetries retries retryable failures with exponential backoff", async () => {
  const delays = [];
  let attempts = 0;
  const result = await withRetries(
    () => {
      attempts += 1;
      if (attempts < 3) {
        const error = new Error("502");
        error.status = 502;
        throw error;
      }
      return "ok";
    },
    { sleep: async (ms) => delays.push(ms) },
  );
  assert.equal(result, "ok");
  assert.equal(attempts, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(delays, [500, 1000]);
});

test("withRetries pauses longer after a rate limit", async () => {
  const delays = [];
  let attempts = 0;
  await withRetries(
    () => {
      attempts += 1;
      if (attempts < 2) {
        const error = new Error("rate limited");
        error.status = 429;
        throw error;
      }
      return "ok";
    },
    { sleep: async (ms) => delays.push(ms) },
  );
  assert.equal(attempts, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(delays, [1200]);
});

test("withRetries does not retry invalid (4xx) queries", async () => {
  let attempts = 0;
  const delays = [];
  await assert.rejects(
    withRetries(
      () => {
        attempts += 1;
        const error = new Error("validation failed");
        error.status = 400;
        throw error;
      },
      { sleep: async (ms) => delays.push(ms) },
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(attempts, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(delays, []);
});

test("withRetries throws after bounded retries and never retries forever", async () => {
  let attempts = 0;
  const delays = [];
  await assert.rejects(
    withRetries(
      () => {
        attempts += 1;
        const error = new Error("still down");
        error.status = 502;
        throw error;
      },
      { retries: 2, sleep: async (ms) => delays.push(ms) },
    ),
    /still down/,
  );
  assert.equal(attempts, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(delays, [500, 1000]);
});

test("out-of-order and duplicate terms produce stable variant sets", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("Wind Breaker"), searchVariants("Wind Breaker"));
  assert.deepEqual(searchVariants("  wind breaker "), searchVariants("wind breaker"));
});

import base44 from "@base44/vite-plugin"
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

      navigationNotifier: true,
